金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 13 日
上週重點訊息
◼ TOPIX 啟動大規模改革：日本交易所集團 10 月 7 日公布 TOPIX 調整結果，符合新制納入標
準的成分股將由 9 月底的 1,636 檔降至 986 檔，另有 35 檔股票新納入。現有成分股中，共
有 683 檔未符合流動性及浮動市值等標準，這些股票將自 2026 年 10 月底起逐步降低權
重，並於 2027 年 10 月重新評估，屆時仍未達標者將於 2028 年 7 月底降至零。此次改革有
助於改善 TOPIX 成分股的流動性及投資價值，並促使企業更加重視市場評價。

◼ 巴西總統選舉波索納洛首輪領先魯拉：巴西最高選舉法院 10 月 5 日公布首輪投票結果，參
議員弗拉維奧・波索納洛取得 47.03%的有效票，現任總統魯拉獲得 45.16%，兩人均未取得
過半票數，將於 10 月 25 日進行第二輪決選。若最終由波索納洛當選將相較有利於巴西股市
表現。
◼ IEA 支持加速釋出既有儲油：國際能源署（IEA）10 月 7 日表示，會員國支持加快釋出 3 月
已承諾提供的儲油，並將優先增加柴油供應。截至目前，會員國已釋出約 3.25 億桶儲油；若
剩餘承諾全數執行，可再向市場提供約 1 億桶供應，有助緩解運輸業與農業面臨的燃料成本
壓力。隨各國持續釋出戰略儲備石油，將有利於在短期內壓抑全球油價進一步上行。
市場展望
◼ 標普 500 獲利預估連續上修，資訊科技為主要推升來源：標普 500 企業獲利預估連續兩季上
修，其中資訊科技產業為主要改善來源，反映 AI、雲端運算及資料中心需求仍然強勁。雖然
美國資料中心仍面臨電力與基礎設施瓶頸，但建置規模持續擴大，AI 投資也逐步延伸至記憶

體、網路設備、資安及企業應用等領域。因此，預期科技類股的獲利動能仍具支撐，具 AI 供
應鏈優勢的台股與日股亦可望延續相關成長動能。
◼ 中天期債券可望兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府與企業發債供給維持高
檔，可能使市場利率維持在較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企業獲利、
現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，較高的美國公債基準利率也使其利息收益維持一定水準。短天
期債券面臨利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較易受到公債供給及利率波動影響，因
此中天期美國公債及投資級債可望在息收與利率風險之間維持較為平衡的表現。

➢ 結論
AI算力需求推升資料中心擴建，電力與散熱成為建置瓶頸
2030年AI Workload容量上看156GW(CAGR 28.8%)；電網補強需3~5年，機房建置僅需1~2年。
接電時程落差推升現地供電需求，SOFC取得大型AIDC訂單
Oracle、Nebius、AEP/Google相繼採用BE燃料電池；2030年SOFC市場機會約22GW，資料中心占49%。
液冷由晶片延伸至整機與電源，規格與價值量同步升級
Rubin世代全面液冷，CDU放大至2.3~3MW；800VDC電源櫃液冷化，快接頭升級至大口徑與ZQD。
看好液冷冷板、快接頭與SOFC關鍵零組件供應鏈
奇鋐(3017)、富世達(6805)受惠液冷規格升級；高力(8996)切入SOFC供應鏈，宏致(3605)列入觀察。

### Page 4

3
AI 算力需求推升科技業者資本支出，帶動資料中心長期擴建
➢ AI算力需求與AIDC建置瓶頸
預估全球AI Workload容量需求將由2025年44GW成長至2030年156GW，CAGR達28.8%，五年新增112GW。顯示AI算力
需求將持續帶動資料中心建置及相關基礎設施投資。
預估北美主要雲端與科技業者(CSP)資本支出將由2025年4,450億美元成長至2028年1.15兆美元，CAGR達37.2%。隨CSP持
續投入算力擴建，將帶動AI伺服器、機櫃、電力及散熱等基礎設施需求，支撐相關供應鏈中長期成長動能。
AI算力需求驅動資料中心擴建，2030年AI Workload容量上看156GW CSP資本支出加速擴張，2028年規模上看1.15兆美元
資料來源：McKinsey & Company、兆豐投顧整理

AIDC 擴建推升用電規模與機櫃密度，供電及散熱能力影響建置進度
➢ AI算力需求與AIDC建置瓶頸
電網擴建與資料中心建置存在時程落差 ，電力取得速度成為AIDC擴張的重要限制。IEA指出，新建電網從規劃、許可至完工
可能需5–15年，資料中心建置則約1–3年。
AI運算朝高密度系統整合，配電與液冷升級需求涵蓋GPU及CSP自研ASIC。除NVIDIA GB200／GB300外，Google TPU 8t
／8i採第四代液冷，AWS Trainium3 Teton3 MAX採冷板液冷架構，AMD Helios亦已有支援最高246kW雙寬機櫃的合作參
考設計，系統驗證與量產交付能力將成為AIDC建置之關鍵。
全球再生能源與大型儲能併網排隊規模逾2,300 GW 高密度GPU與ASIC機櫃功率上升將持續推動配電及液冷升級
資料來源：IEA、兆豐投顧整理

### Page 6

5
電網補強與機房建置存在時程落差，提升AIDC現地供電需求
➢ AI算力需求與AIDC建置瓶頸
AIDC擴張需同步取得發電供應、輸配電容量及場址受電能力，任一環節受限，皆可能影響機房投運時程。隨單一場址用電規
模擴大，部分專案需新增變電站 、饋線及相關設備，供電進度因而受到重電設備交期拉長 、區域電網併網與饋線容量不足 、
熱損耗與轉換效率等因素影響。
NLR指出，美國資料中心建置與負載爬坡通常約需12–24個月，公用事業電網補強則常需3–5年。我們認為，兩者的時程落差
將提高業者評估分階段供電及現地發電的意願 ，並為燃料電池與燃氣機組等方案創造導入機會；實際效益仍須綜合評估燃料 、
許可、可靠度與完整供電成本。
AIDC供電涉及多個環節，場址接電能力影響投運時程 資料中心(AIDC)建置與電網補強時程比較

### Page 7

6
接電時程落差推升現地供電需求，SOFC提供模組化連續供電選項
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
當電網補強時程無法配合機房投運 ，業者可評估現地發電，搭配分階段送電與儲能等方式銜接供電需求 。方案選擇須綜合考
量連續供電能力、燃料供應、建置條件與可靠度要求。
Bloom Energy採用固態氧化物燃料電池(SOFC)，透過電化學反應將燃料轉換為電力，可依需求配置模組，提供持續性現地
電源。其應用可涵蓋與電網並聯或微電網架構，實際部署仍須配合燃料、許可及場內電力系統設計。
現地發電增加機房供電來源 現地發電與儲能方案比較
資料來源：Bloom Energy、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源：DOE、Cummins、Caterpillar、BE、兆豐投顧整理
註：本圖簡化供電路徑，電力轉換、UPS、儲能與備援配置依案場設計
方案 主要功能 選用時的評估重點
電池儲能 電力銜接 與功率調節 儲能量體、輸出功率及充電來源
柴油發電機 停電備援 依額定選擇運轉用途 燃料補給、設備額定及適用許可
燃氣渦輪 可配置為 持續發電來源 天然氣供應與工程、運轉條件

SOFC燃料電池 模組化配置 持續發電 燃料供應、高溫啟停及電堆維護
註：實際用途依額定與案場設計；天然氣SOFC仍有CO2排放

### Page 8

7
SOFC以電化學反應直接發電，模組化整合支援資料中心現地供電
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
BE採固態氧化物燃料電池 (SOFC)，氧離子穿過固態電解質與燃料進行電化學反應直接發電 ，電子經外部迴路流動 ，形成直
流電，不經燃燒，電效率達 53–65%。SOFC由多片電池組成電堆 ，再整合為發電模組，搭配燃料處理、空氣供應、熱管理
與電力轉換設備，形成完整供電系統。
一座1GW的資料中心採用濕式冷卻燃氣渦輪機聯合循環發電系統 ，在其使用壽命期間，大約會消耗250億加侖的水；但若是
用燃料電池僅需140萬加侖。
氧離子穿越陶瓷電解質，電子經外部迴路形成電力 電池片逐級整合為電堆、發電模組與供電系統
資料來源：IEA、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源：AEP、兆豐投顧整理

### Page 9

8
發電效率與部署條件，決定SOFC在AIDC的採用機會
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
AIDC持續運轉將累積大量燃料支出 ，提高發電效率可降低相同電力輸出所需的天然氣量 。SOFC具高效率發電特性，其經濟
價值須結合燃料價格、使用時數及維護成本評估。
SOFC、燃氣引擎與燃氣渦輪各有部署及運轉條件 。業主須共同評估設備交付 、場址工程、燃氣供應、排放許可及維運需求 ，
才能判斷適合的現地供電方案。
資料來源：兆豐投顧試算 資料來源：DOE、GE、Caterpillar、BE、兆豐投顧整理
相同電力輸出下，提高效率可降低燃料需求 現地供電方案須兼顧效率、擴建與運轉維護

### Page 10

9
政策支援燃料電池投資，木星計畫燃氣與許可牽動落地時程
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
木星計畫位於美國新墨西哥州 ，為Oracle支援OpenAI Stargate建置的AI資料中心園區。Oracle於2026年4月宣布，規劃採
用最高2.45GW的Bloom Energy燃料電池裝置容量，取代原先燃氣輪機與柴油發電機方案，展現對SOFC現地供電的需求。
木星計畫的SOFC方案仍以天然氣供應為前提，燃氣接入與空氣品質許可均影響建置進度。州土地辦公室7月維持拒發特定管
線通行權等申請，Oracle於10月另提出微電網設計補件，顯示大型專案須同步協調設備建置、燃料供應與案場許可。
美國 OBBBA 將合格燃料電池納入 Section 48E 投資稅額抵減 甲骨文木星計劃微電網建置須經許可審查與方案調整
資料來源：GPO、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源：NMED、NMSLO、Oracle、兆豐投顧整理
項目 內容

抵減比例 合格投資基礎金額 x 30%

排放條件 專屬條款排除一般設施的溫室氣體排放資格門檻
加成限制 此項30%比例不得疊加其他加成

### Page 11

10
大型AIDC合作案擴大，BE交付與擴產節奏成為供應鏈觀察重點
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
Oracle、Nebius等業者已公告採用BE燃料電池，顯示SOFC已取得大型AIDC商業合作。評估需求規模時，須分別辨識合作
上限、已簽約容量及實際部署進度。
機組製造、場址工程與許可程序須共同推進。供應鏈需求應依BE製造擴產、設備出貨及案場交付節奏追蹤，並將融資合作框
架與設備訂單分開評估。
BE大型合作案涵蓋設備採購、供電服務與專案融資 BE製造產能隨現地供電需求擴張，2027年起每年增加逾2GW

### Page 12

11
SOFC擴建帶動高溫熱管理與電力線纜需求，台廠切入不同供應環節
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
依Ceres基於BNEF資料之分析 ，2030年全球SOFC市場機會約22GW，其中資料中心應用占 49%(約10.8GW)，為最大單一
應用市場。隨AI資料中心擴建與供電需求提升 ，資料中心可望成為SOFC商業化的重要需求來源 ，帶動高溫熱管理與電力零
組件的應用機會。
SOFC系統需要高溫熱交換 、電力轉換與系統配線等零組件 。高力提供熱反應器 、複熱器等高溫熱管理關鍵零組件；宏致則
供應高溫反應模組耐高溫電纜、電力控制與轉換模組內電纜，以及系統管理與電力傳輸線纜。
Ceres預估2030年全球SOFC市場機會達22GW，其中資料中心占49% 325 kW Energy Server 的組件與運作方式

### Page 13

12
供電與液冷協同升級，支撐AIDC算力密度與能源效率提升
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
在總供電及機房空間受限下，AIDC須兼顧能源與空間使用效率。降低冷卻與配電等基礎設施耗能，可在相同總用電條件下提
高IT設備用電占比；評估整體效益時，除PUE外，仍須觀察單位運算耗電及單位面積算力。
液冷透過冷板、分歧管與CDU將熱帶至設施端，高壓直流與Power sidecar則調整供電及設備配置。隨散熱需求延伸至電源
模組，供電與冷卻系統須協同設計 ，兼顧電力轉換、散熱耗能及部署密度。而電源模組採用液冷，全新的水冷資料中心機房
即不再需要傳統的冷熱通道(Hot/Cold Aisle)與大型空調系統，基礎設施CAPEX預估可節省高達10%。
相同總用電下，降低基礎設施耗能可提高IT用電占比 運算與電源同步液冷，降低對機房氣流散熱的依賴
資料來源：DOE、兆豐投顧試算 資料來源：NVIDIA、MEGMEET、兆豐投顧整理
度/電
註：依PUE定義進行情境試算，非特定液冷方案實測結果。IT設備用

電包含伺服器內部風扇等耗電，不等同晶片運算用電。

熱捕捉範圍擴大，液冷設計由主晶片延伸至整機架構
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
除CPU、GPU、TPU及ASIC等主要熱源外，液冷進一步涵蓋DIMM、OSFP光模組與板級元件，透過冷板及導熱板提高液體
帶走熱量的比例，降低對氣流散熱的依賴。
為支援不同熱源與機構配置 ，運算主板與整機伺服器之液冷解決方案多元化 ，包括冷板迴路式、混合式及整合式大冷板架構 。
我們認為，隨液冷涵蓋元件增加，流道與連接設計、模組組裝及可靠度驗證的重要性將提高 ，供應鏈機會亦由單顆冷板延伸
至整機冷卻整合。
液冷涵蓋主晶片與周邊元件，熱捕捉比例最高達100% 冷板迴路式、混合式與整合式架構支援不同整機配置
資料來源：CoolIT、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源： CoolIT、兆豐投顧整理

### Page 15

CDU串接IT與設施冷卻迴路，承接液冷擴張後的排熱需求
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
隨液冷由CPU、GPU延伸至記憶體、光模組及板級元件，液體承擔的設備熱量增加；機櫃功率密度提高 ，更進一步推升液冷
迴路的排熱需求。CDU串接IT側與設施側，透過熱交換器、循環泵浦與控制系統維持供液條件 ，其換熱能力與供液穩定性將
成為高密度AIDC的重要設計環節。
CDU升級除提高散熱容量，更須兼顧能源與空間使用效率 。OCP 2026的2.3MW系統模擬研究顯示，在相同散熱負載下，比
較方案間全年冷卻系統能耗差距約17%。
冷板取熱、CDU換熱、設施端排熱，構成完整液冷迴路 相同2.3MW散熱負載之下，CDU選型仍牽動流量與全年能耗

註：典型液對液CDU示意圖。紅、藍代表各迴路相對高低溫，非特定
案場溫度。

Deschutes開放2MW級CDU設計，瞄準能源與空間效率提升
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
Google將第五代 CDU設計Deschutes貢獻予OCP，提供高密度 AI機房可採用的開放規格 。其v1.0設計以2MW散熱容量 、

浦及溫度、壓力與流量監控，兼顧容量與供液可靠度。我們認為，CDU的競爭重點將涵蓋單位占地容量 、低溫差換熱及泵浦
耗能，須從整體冷卻系統評估其能源與空間效益。

800VDC重整供電配置，液冷延伸至高密度電源櫃
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
隨AIDC機櫃功率提高，低電壓供電面臨大電流、導體體積與電源設備占用空間等挑戰。Power sidecar將前端電力轉換與部
分備援設備移至獨立電源櫃 ，再以800VDC送至運算櫃，並依架構於負載附近降壓；透過提高輸電電壓與調整設備配置 ，支
援更高功率密度部署。
NVIDIA第二代MGX Power Rack參考設計，朝100%液冷發展，並標示880kW供電能力(N配置、含BBU)及90秒備援供電時
間。隨電力轉換與備援設備集中，液冷需求將進一步延伸至電源模組，使供電與散熱成為能源及空間效率升級的重要環節。
前端電源移至獨立機櫃，800VDC支援高功率密度供電 NVIDIA MGX第二代電源櫃朝全液冷發展，液冷散熱模組與零組件用量提升

液冷導入電源模組，推進功率密度與能源效率升級
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
電源模組導入液冷後，可透過冷板將功率元件的熱量帶至冷卻液 ，降低對風扇、大型散熱鰭片與風道的需求 。相較風冷設計，
液冷有助縮小散熱結構占用空間，並降低風扇耗電及元件工作溫度，使電源模組朝更高功率密度發展。
MEGMEET於2026年10月OCP展示100kW、800V液冷AC/DC電源層，採2RU不含BBU設計，產品標示轉換效率逾98%；另
展示1RU、95kW的800V轉54V液冷DC/DC電源層。我們認為，液冷應用延伸至整流與降壓環節 ，將擴大電源冷板、流道設
計及模組整合的需求，提升水冷板、快接頭、滑軌、機殼與液冷busbar之需求量。
水冷板承接功率元件熱量，降低電源模組對風扇與風道的依賴 液冷電源層涵蓋前端整流與櫃內降壓，支援緊湊供電配置

晶片TDP與出貨量同步提升，水冷板技術與ASP仍具升級空間
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
晶片TDP逐代提升，NVIDIA由GB300的1.4kW增至Rubin 2.3kW、Rubin Ultra約2.6kW，TPU v9與Trainium4將達3~4kW。

石銅等材料升級持續解熱。

顆、占比由37%升至55%，超越GPU；出貨放量加上電源櫃與光模組導入液冷，水冷板用量與滲透率仍有提升空間。
TDP持續攀升，水冷板技術與ASP仍有升級空間 AI晶片出貨量預估，整體供應鏈於2027年仍供不應求

冷板與流體零組件升級，須兼顧散熱效能與系統耗電
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
晶片功耗提升推高冷板流量需求 ，惟CDU可供IT側使用的壓差有限(Deschutes規格為80~90psi)，冷板與快接頭須以更低壓
降輸送更大流量。Rubin Ultra將導入內建球閥、提升流量的ZQD；我們認為，快接頭技術門檻由防漏延伸至大流量低壓降，
帶動規格與單價升級。
NVIDIA MGX第二代電源櫃全面液冷，以1座電源櫃搭配2座運算櫃為一組試算，每組新增26片電源冷板及52顆快接頭；惟兩
段電源轉換熱損合計僅約供電量4%，增量以件數為主，價值量約為運算櫃快接頭的5%。
電源設備液冷化：每組(1*電源櫃+2*運算櫃)零組件用量試算 流量提升推動快接頭規格升級，Rubin Ultra每tray 10顆MQD改採ZQD

### Page 22

21
液冷由晶片延伸至整機與電源，單櫃價值倍增帶動台廠受惠
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
液冷由CPU、GPU延伸至記憶體、光模組及電源設備，Rubin世代全面採用100%液冷，CDU容量放大至2.3~3MW。我們預
估，單櫃液冷價值(含CDU)由約10萬美元倍增至約20萬美元，全球液冷市場將由2026年105億美元成長至2030年853億美元
(CAGR約69%)。
價值升級沿三條主線：CDU放大容量與泵浦冗餘、冷板導入雙面水冷與鑽石銅、快接頭朝大口徑低壓降及ZQD升級；電源櫃
液冷則以件數貢獻增量。我們認為，冷板與快接頭兼具規格升級與用量成長，奇鋐、雙鴻、富世達最直接受惠。
全球液冷市場2030年達853億美元，二次側占約7成5 液冷各環節升級方向與單櫃價值量占比

### Page 23

22
結論
➢ 結論
AI算力需求推升資料中心擴建，電力與散熱成為建置瓶頸
2030年AI Workload容量上看156GW(CAGR 28.8%)；電網補強需3~5年，機房建置僅需1~2年。
接電時程落差推升現地供電需求，SOFC取得大型AIDC訂單
Oracle、Nebius、AEP/Google相繼採用BE燃料電池；2030年SOFC市場機會約22GW，資料中心占49%。
液冷由晶片延伸至整機與電源，規格與價值量同步升級
Rubin世代全面液冷，CDU放大至2.3~3MW；800VDC電源櫃液冷化，快接頭升級至大口徑與ZQD。
看好液冷冷板、快接頭與SOFC關鍵零組件供應鏈
奇鋐(3017)、富世達(6805)受惠液冷規格升級；高力(8996)切入SOFC供應鏈，宏致(3605)列入觀察。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 澳洲央行決議如預期升息一碼至 4.60%為今年第四度升息，基準利率創 2011 年
以來新高，澳洲央行在政策聲明中表示通膨壓力尚未緩解，不排除在11、12 月
政策會議進一步升息；另一方面，澳洲8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)月增 0.4%略低
於預期，但年增 4.0%仍高於澳洲央行 2~3%的目標區間。
⚫ 美國第二季美 GDP 終值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%， 其中實質個人消費支出創下2025
年 3 月以來最大單月增幅，顯示即使高利率持續，美國家庭消費依然具韌性；
另一方面，美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增月增 0.2%、3.0%，雙雙低於市場預
期的 0.3%、3.3%，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人，失
業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，8 月非農就業人數亦同步下修至 13.3 萬人，通膨下滑
和勞動力市場略有降溫，降低市場對聯準會 10 月升息預期，Fedwatch 顯示 10

月升息機率下降至 20%左右。
⚫ 受美伊衝突引發的能源價格衝擊影響 ，9 月歐元地區消費者物價調和指數(HICP)
整體上升速度遠超預期，其中，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%，歐元區年增
3.8%，均高於歐洲央行的 2%目標，反映能源價格的衝擊持續影響物價，加深歐
洲央行再次升息的壓力。
⚫ 日本上週公佈貨幣政策會議紀要及央行意見摘要顯示多名委員認為當前政策利
率依然處於中性區間之下 ，需警惕通膨上行風險 ， 且未來應該以更快速度升息抑
制通膨，然而意見摘要提及日本經濟財政大臣城內實表示希望央行充分審視過
去升息對景氣之影響 ，市場解讀為政府牽制日本央行未來升息步伐 ， 而財務大臣
片山皋月則重申尊重日本央行的自主性。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.760，低點在 31.946，以 31.830 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方，市場預期聯準
會後續仍有升息的空間，提振美元指數表現，加以外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣走貶
至 31.900 上方；不過適逢月底也是季底，匯價逢高吸引出口商進場賣美金，緩解台
幣貶值的速度；臨近週末，美國 8 月核心 PCE 數據 3%低於預期 3.3%，但市場等待
9 月非農就業數據，交投偏向謹慎觀望，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.830。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略有
減弱，市場對於 10 月升息的預期下降，但仍預期 12 月會再度升息。美元指數雖然
脫離高點，但仍在 102 附近高位震盪，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台股再創收盤新高且外資轉為買超台股，且隨著 AI 需求持續帶動台灣經濟出口表
現強勁，預計出口商後續拋匯需求將持續提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，預估本週台幣
區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪偏升。隨著美中兩國完成新一輪談判，雙方同意延長休兵，從
原定的 11 月延長到明年 1 月 ， 降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險 ， 支撐人民幣的走勢由
6.7170 走升至 6.7030；不過由於美伊戰事持續 ，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方 ，
加以美元指數及美債收益率雙雙走高 ， 令人民幣由升轉貶至6.7200 上方 ； 臨近週末，
美國公布 9 月非農數據降溫，聯準會10 月升息預期下滑，美元指數回落，境外人民
幣走升至 6.7000 週內高點，終場境外人民幣收在 6.7061，週線升值 166 點，境內人
民幣於十一長假前夕收盤於在 6.7053，週線升值 72 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週人民幣區間震盪 。 就國際市場來看 ， 美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，遠低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略
為減弱。數據公布後，市場對於聯準會10 月升息的預期下降，美元指數小幅回落，
有助於人民幣的表現；就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價格再度升破 6.7400，有助
於人民幣的走勢 ， 不過10/1~10/7 是中國十一長假 ， 預計人民幣走勢主要將受國際美
元影響；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線仍向下發散，空頭格
局不變，不過匯價反彈至 20 日均線附近，顯示下行動能暫緩。綜合以上，預估本週
人民幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7300

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 震盪上行。週初，美元指數持穩在 101 以上高位，USD/JPY 大致
在 156.50 至 157.85 間波動；週間，適逢日本上半年會計年度結算，出口商拋匯使資
金匯回力道強勁 ， 帶動USD/JPY 下行最低來到 156.35，但逢低有買盤進場，USD/JPY
回到 157 整數關卡之上，後隨美債殖利率創高、美元指數突破 102 關卡，USD/JPY
上行最高來至 158.45；臨近週末，美國公布 9 月非農就業人數低於預期，USD/JPY
一度回落至 157 下方，終場收在 157.83，週線上漲 57 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪 ， 主要原因如下 ： 一 、日本 9 月製造業 PMI 由 54.9
回落至 54.1，增長速度放慢至六個月以來的低位，主因為產出和新訂單增長放緩，
經濟復甦力道可能減緩，不利日圓表現。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%，
美國 9 月非農數據 2.9 萬人均低於預期，通膨和勞動力市場略有降溫使市場對聯準
會升息預期下滑，加之部分聯準會官員表示無需急於進一步升息，Fedwatch 顯示 10
月升息機率降至兩成，然而美國第二季 GDP 上修至 2.2%經濟基本面穩健仍將支撐
美元。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價處於區間波動，且各天期均線
趨平，上方 158.50 有年線、季線壓力，下方 156.25 則有月線支撐，預期短線以區間
震盪可能性較高。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪。

2026年 7-9月期 GDP(1次速報)予測(改訂版) 
～前期比年率＋1.0％に下方修正～ 
直近公表の基礎統計を踏まえ、外需を下方修正

経済調査部 エコノミスト 畑中 宏仁 
  チーフエコノミスト 神田 慶司

[要約] 
◼ 11月16日公表予定の2026年7-9月期のGDP1次速報では、実質GDP成長率は前期比年
率＋1.0％（前期比＋0.2％）と、4四半期連続のプラス成長になると予想する。10月
30日時点では、前期比年率＋1.5％（前期比＋0.4％）を予想していたが、その後公表
された基礎統計の結果を踏まえ、予測値を下方修正した。 
◼ 改訂後の予測では、2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、個人消費は小幅に減少、輸出も減
少したものの、設備投資の増加に支えられプラス成長になったとみる。10-12 月期は、
個人消費が小幅に増加し、設備投資と輸出も増加するものの、輸入の伸びによって外
需寄与度がマイナスとなり、実質GDPはおおむね横ばいになるとみている。

2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP成長率予想を前期比年率＋1.0％に下方修正 
11月 16日公表予定の 2026年 7-9月期の GDP1次速報では、実質 GDP成長率は前期比年率＋
1.0％（前期比＋0.2％）と、4四半期連続のプラス成長になると予想する。10月 30日時点で
は、前期比年率＋1.5％（前期比＋0.4％）を予想していた1が、その後公表された基礎統計の結
果を踏まえ、予測値を下方修正した。 
11月 10日に公表された家計調査や国際収支統計などの結果を反映し、個人消費の予測値を
前期比▲0.1％から同▲0.0％に上方修正した。10月30日時点と同じく、耐久財と、半耐久財、
非耐久財はいずれも減少、サービスは増加を予想する。国内家計最終消費支出の修正幅は小さ
かったものの、控除項目であるインバウンドの下方修正が民間最終消費支出を押し上げた。 
また、国際収支統計の結果を反映し、輸出の予測値を前期比＋0.5％から同▲0.5％に下方修
正し、輸入の予測値を前期比＋1.8％から同＋1.9％に上方修正した。10月 30日時点の予想と
比べ、輸出は財・サービスともに下方修正し、輸入は財・サービスともに上方修正した。この
結果、外需寄与度の予測値を前期比▲0.2％ptから同▲0.4％ptに下方修正した。 
そのほか、10月 30日正午以降に公表された基礎統計の結果を受けて、住宅投資を前期比▲
1.8％から同▲1.6％、設備投資を前期比＋2.0％から同＋2.3％、公共投資を前期比▲1.0％か
ら同▲0.8％にそれぞれ上方修正した。 
改訂後の予測では、2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、個人消費が小幅に減少し、輸出も4-6月
期に大幅に増加した研究開発サービスの反動減もあって減少したものの、設備投資の増加に支
えられ、プラス成長になったとみる。10-12月期については、個人消費は小幅に増加し、設備
投資と輸出もAI関連需要を背景に増加するものの、輸入の伸びによって外需寄与度がマイナス
となり、実質 GDPは前期比でおおむね横ばいになると見込んでいる。リスク要因は中東情勢の
悪化とAI関連需要の減速である。中東情勢が悪化し、原油価格がさらに高騰すれば、物価高を
通じて個人消費を一段と下押しするだろう。また、足元の米国経済では設備投資が景気を下支
えしており、とりわけデータセンターや半導体などのIT関連投資がけん引役となっているが、
AI関連企業の設備投資が鈍化すれば、米国向け輸出が下押しされる可能性があり、注意が必要
だ。

1 需要項目ごとの説明を含む詳細は、畑中宏仁・神田慶司「2026年 7-9月期 GDP（1次速報）予測～前期比年
率＋1.5％を予想～」（大和総研レポート、2026年10月 30日）を参照。

### Page 1

WT Microelectronics

Datacenter continues to fuel growth amid 
a less favorable funding surrounding

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 270.00 TWD | Price: 195.00 TWD

3Q26 ahead on operating leverage  
3Q26 net income NT$10.3bn was 4% ahead of BofAe/consensus, thanks mainly to a 
slightly stronger-than-expected operating leverage. By end markets, the sequential 
revenue momentum turned out to be stronger-than-expected across auto, industrial, CE 
(consumer electronics), PC (personal computer) and smartphone verticals in 3Q26, due 
partly to the earlier pull-in which also will likely lead to a QoQ revenue drop in 4Q26 for 
these segments.  
Reaccelerating datacenter business into 4Q26 and 2027 
4Q26 revenue is guided to grow by 25+% QoQ based on the mid-point of guidance 
range, thanks mainly to the reacceleration in datacenter-related business, which more 
than offset the QoQ revenue decline in the segments mentioned above. Yet we expect 
the firm’s interest expense to notably increase in 4Q26 (vs 3Q26) to NT$3bn+, while 
management also attributes such increase to a higher funding cost (interest rate) 
environment and a bigger amount of working capital required to fuel future business 
growth. Looking into 2027, WT Micro expects continuous growth across datacenter, auto 
and industrial areas.   
Lower PO on a lowered multiple given higher interest rate 
We raise 2026-28E EPS by 7-11% after baking in the assumptions of stronger operating 
leverage post the release of 3Q26/4Q26 results/guidance, while our PO goes to NT$270 
(from NT$330) now based on 7x (was 10x) 2H27-1H28E P/E, at the low-to-mid-end of 
its historical 2011-25 trading range of 4-15x, we believe this level of PE is warranted 
because of a higher funding cost (interest rate at 4+%) environment into 2027-28 
balanced by a growing op margin, ROE and EPS CAGR over the forecast period. Given 
the still undemanding valuation at 6x/5x P/E and 11%/14% dividend yield in 2027/28E, 
we reiterate Buy rating on the stock, while staying confident for the company to record 
20+% top-line/bottom-line CAGR during 2026-28E.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
WT Microelectronics was founded in 1993 and went listed 
in 2002. The firm is the biggest distributor for 
semiconductor and technology hardware components 
globally with 10+% market share, and with more than 25k 
customers and over 400 suppliers. WT Micro operates in 
dual-headquarter business model, after acquiring Future 
Electronics in 2024, and now it has over eight thousand 
employees with over 160 sales branches.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on WT Micro, as we like the structural 
growth in datacenter business mainly driven by TPU, as well 
as the emerging benefits/synergies from the cross-selling 
with Future Electronics.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
WT Microelectronics (XZOPF) 
We base our PO NT$270 on 7x 2H27-1H28E P/E, which is at the low-to-mid-end of its 
historical 2011-25 trading range of 4-15x. This is justified in our view, given the higher 
interest rate balanced by the support from: 1) an operating margin of 2.2-2.3% in 2027-
28E, 2) improving ROE toward 33% in 2027-28E (vs 12% in 2024-25), and 3) 22% EPS 
CAGR during 2026-28E.

Upside risks to our PO are: 
1) faster-than-expected demand recovery in auto/industrial verticals, especially from 
US/Europe market 
2) a lower interest rate that could alleviate the company's financial burden at non-
operating level.

Downside risks to our PO are: 
1) a revenue decline in datacenter/server related business, which has been the key 
growth driver during 2021-24 
2) market share loss as suppliers or customers seek other distributors' help after WT 
Micro consolidates Future Electronics. 
3) Potential overhang from share dilution.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 -  Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation). 
Investment rating Total return expectation (within 12-month period of date of initial 
rating)

INCOME RATINGS, indicators of potential cash dividends, are: 7 - same/higher (dividend considered to be secure), 8 - same/lower (dividend not considered to be 
secure) and 9 - pays no cash dividend.  Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two or more analysts sharing a common industry, 
sector, region or other classification(s). A stock’ s coverage cluster is included in the most recent BofA Global Research report referencing the stock.

個股報告 
富世達(6805) 
伺服器占比突破八成，QD量價齊揚推升獲利 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $3000 
前次投資建議 2026.08.05: 
「買進」 ，目標價: $2360

報告日期：2026.11.04 研 究 員：陳威仲

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 26Q3 毛利率優於預期，伺服器產品營收占比升至 81.3%，上修 2026/27 年 EPS 至
65.5/120.1 元(前次預估 64.3/112.8元)， 目標價由2360 元調升至 3000 元(25X 27(F)PER)，
維持【買進】投資評等。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/11/3 收盤價： 2160.00 ➢ 26Q3 營收 45.0 億元 (QoQ+20.4%/YoY+26.2%)，受
VR200、ASIC 平台小量拉貨與非奇鋐之快接頭客戶出貨
增加帶動；毛利率 38.0%(季增 3.9ppts/年增 10.3ppts，
優於預期 2.9ppts)，主因伺服器產品營收占比升至
81.3%；營業淨利 14.6 億元(QoQ+39.9%/YoY+84.2%)；
稅後淨利 12.4億元(QoQ+46.3%/YoY+80.8%)，EPS 18.1
元。 
➢ QD 受惠 GPU 與 ASIC 雙平台放量及規格升級，Rubin 
Ultra 將導入單價約兩倍的 ZQD，AWS Trn3 等 ASIC 專
案將於 2027 年陸續貢獻；AI 滑軌亦陸續通過驗證，27
年可望隨 VR200與 ASIC專案放量。 
➢ 華為摺疊機新品拉貨遞延，加上記憶體漲價影響終端需
求 ， 下 修26Q4 營 收 預 估 至54.0 億元
(QoQ+20.0%/YoY+35.8%)；惟伺服器產品比重上升，毛
利率上修至 38.1%，EPS 21.63元。 
➢ 預估 2026/27 年 EPS 65.5/120.1 元，將持續受惠強勁的
伺服器需求與水冷滲透率提升之下帶動獲利成長，目前股
價分別位於 2026/27 年 EPS的 33.0/18.0X，過去股價區
間落在 15~87X，目標價 3000 元(25X 27(F)PER)，維持
【買進】投資評等，潛在上漲空間 38.9%。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

富世達為 AI基礎設施與高階摺疊機轉軸的關鍵供應商 
富世達股份有限公司成立於民國 2001 年，為奇鋐(3017)持股 16.87%的轉投
資公司，總部與研發中心位於新北新莊，並於越南、中國深圳設有生產基地，
提供各類金屬沖壓設計與製造，主要產品為伺服器組件(滑軌、水冷快接頭)，
與摺疊手機、筆記型電腦、平板電腦之轉軸零件，從消費性電子零組件廠轉
型為 AI基礎設施與高階摺疊機轉軸的關鍵供應商，主要出貨終端包含華為、
Motorola 等摺疊機，與各大AI伺服器平台(NVIDIA、AMD、ASIC等)機櫃用
的滑軌與水冷快接頭。

資料來源：富世達、兆豐投顧整理與預估

26Q3 伺服器產品營收占比上升至 81.3%，帶動毛利率大幅優於預期。 
富世達 26Q3 營收 45.0 億元(QoQ+20.4%/YoY+26.2%，優於兆豐前次預期
4%)，因 VR200、ASIC 平台小量拉貨與非奇鋐之快接頭客戶規模上升使伺
服器快接頭營收增長強勁；毛利率 38.0%(季增 3.9ppts/年增 10.3ppts，優於
兆豐預期 2.9ppts)，主因產品組合改善，伺服器產品營收占比超越 80%；營
業淨利 14.6 億元 (QoQ+39.9%/YoY+84.2%)， 稅 後 淨 利12.4 億元
(QoQ+46.3%/YoY+80.8%)，EPS 18.1元(前次預估 15.9 元)。

資料來源：Cmoney、兆豐投顧整理與預估

QD 受惠 GPU與 ASIC雙平台放量及規格升級，為伺服器產品主要成長動能 
26Q3 富世達伺服器產品占營收 81.3%，其中快接頭(QD)約 90%，整體單季
稼動率約 80~90%；而目前新產能開出後 QD 產能已達 1350 萬顆/季。今年
QD 營收貢獻以 GPU 平台為主，明年 ASIC 比重將持續提高，預期 2028 年
ASIC 比重有望超越 GPU；其中(1)AWS Trn3 已配合客戶時程出貨，將於
27H1顯著貢獻營收。(2)Google TPU V9世代有望由現行的 NSP1接頭，變
為導入混用 QD+NSP1接頭，以優化未來光模塊、DIMM 等部件潛在導入水
冷系統後維護的容易性。(3)Meta MTIA 與微軟 MAIA 等 AISC 專案也將於
27H2 出貨帶動 QD 市場規模提升。而在 Rubin Ultra 世代，將導入 ZQD 於
運算托盤(取代部份的 UQD)與 rack manifold之快接頭，且ZQD單顆價值預
估將為現行 MQD與 UQD 單價的兩倍，ZQD 預計 12月進入量產準備。 
規格面，VR200 單櫃 QD 數量約 538 顆(含 rack manifold 88 顆)，Rubin 
Ultra(VR300)將為 574~664顆，預估 QD量價齊揚趨勢延續，為2027~2028
年主要成長動能。

AI 滑軌陸續通過驗證，27年可望隨 VR200與 ASIC 專案量產放量 
滑軌事業目前月產能維持 35 萬套，目前以通用型為主，稼動率約 3 成；隨
明年 AI專案的量產，稼動率可望提升至50~60%以上，毛利率也將隨著 ASIC
水冷專案的放量同步改善，水冷櫃滑軌對承重、緩衝與平行度要求較高，ASP
亦較高，公司預期明年滑軌維持高度成長，後續將視訂單規劃擴廠。

- 4 - 
個股報告

華為摺疊機新品拉貨遞延與記憶體推升價格，轉軸營收短期承壓 
華為新機種受記憶體影響拉貨延後，加上終端需求轉弱，26Q3富世達手機
軸承營收占比降至 15.5%，預期26Q4手機轉軸拉貨動能有限。而華為 26H1
中國摺疊機市占逾 7成，摺疊機累計出貨突破 2,000 萬支；惟記憶體漲價使
單機平均成本增加約 200 美元，9/1 起主力機型最高調漲 17%，後續產品將
朝輕薄化發展並導入新材料，每代價格將重新議價，下一代機種仍由富世達
主供。雖蘋果 iPhone Duo 上市帶動摺疊機話題，將使摺疊機長期接受度與
滲透率同步提高，但短期消費性電子仍受記憶體價格推升單價而影響終端購
買意願。

26Q4 營收下修、毛利率上修，獲利預估小幅調降 
預估富世達 26Q4 營收 54.0 億元(QoQ+20.0%/YoY+35.8%，較原估下修
8.2%，主因摺疊機軸承拉貨不如預期 )；毛利率 38.1%(季增 0.1ppts/年增
9.0ppts，較原估上修 1.1ppts，主因 QD 生產進入規模化，帶動高毛利伺服
器比重上升)；稅後淨利 14.8 億元(QoQ+19.4%/YoY+97.3%，較原估下修
4.7%)，EPS 21.63元(原估 22.7 元)。

富世達將持續受惠強勁的伺服器需求與水冷滲透率提升之下帶動獲利成長 
預估 2026/27 年 EPS 65.5/120.1元(前次預估 64.3/112.8元)，預估在多項
AI伺服器專案導入水冷，與後續電源、光模組等部件皆將導入 100%水冷，
2027~2028 年水冷散熱滲透率將大幅上升，帶動富世達伺服器營收持續增長 
，目前股價分別位於 2026/27 年 EPS的 33.0/18.0X，過去股價區間落在
15~87X，目標價由 2360 元(25X 27(F)PER)調整至 3000 元(25X 
27(F)PER)，維持【買進】投資評等，潛在上漲空間 38.9%。

 投資評價與建議 
維持買進評等，目標價 3000 元：3Q26 獲利略優於預期，2027 年成長動能明確，
Vera Rubin Ultra 導入 ZQD 規格 ， 單櫃水冷頭的產值預期增加50%以上 ， 且ASIC
專案於 2027 年放量，預估將持續貢獻至 2028 年，成長動能延續，預估富世達
2026/2027/2028 稅後 EPS 63.51 元/100.53 元/142.00 元，維持買進評等，目標
價維持 3000 元(約當 2027 年 EPS*PER30X)。

### Page 2

2

公司簡介：富世達成立於民國 90 年，母公司為上市公司奇鋐(3017 TT)，公司提供
各類金屬沖壓設計與製造，主要產品為摺疊手機、筆記型電腦、平板電腦之轉軸零
件，近年跨入伺服器領域。3Q26 營收比重：摺疊手機鉸鍊約佔 15.5%，SERVER
相關約佔 81.3%。  
3Q26 本業略優於預期：3Q26 營收 45.02 億(+20.4%QoQ、+26.2%YoY)，受惠
於產品組合、製程改善與自動化導入，3Q26 毛利率達 38%，表現優於預期，營業
利益 14.6 億元，稅後 EPS 18.12 元。 
4Q26 展望持續正向：展望 4Q26 雖然摺疊手機部分，因為記憶體缺貨，終端產品
需求不佳等因素出貨不如預期，但隨著 NVIDIA VR200 與各大專案陸續放量，公
司對整體營運持續維持正向看法 ， 預期伺服器相關出貨將持續增加且營收佔比預期
將維持在高檔； 同時受惠於高毛利的伺服器產品比重提升、製程持續優化與自動化
導入，整體毛利率可望獲得持續改善。預估4Q26 營收 49.66 億元(+10.3%QoQ，
+24.88%YoY)，毛利率 38.15%，稅後 EPS 19.65 元。 
2027 年~2028 年成長動能不虞匱乏：展望 2027 年主要成長動能包括：(1) 預期
對 AWS 的出貨將於 1Q27 開始明顯增溫，Meta、Google、微軟等 CSP 大廠皆於
其 AI 伺服器導入水冷方案 ， 出貨量可望於2027 年放大且貢獻全年營收 ；(2) VR200
已開始出貨，除了浮動接頭持續出貨外，水冷接頭應用由 switch tray 延伸至
computer tray 與 manifold，且營收貢獻延續至 2027 年全年。(3) Vera Rubin 
Ultra swith tray 內 QD 用量由 4 顆變 8 顆，computer tray 相關有 10 顆 MQD
改為 ZQD 有利 ASP 提升(預期較目前 ASP 高一倍以上)，研究處評估整體 Vera 
Rubin ultra 水冷頭產值將較 Vera Rubin 進一步提升 50%以上，順利的將可於
2H27 開始貢獻營收，且貢獻度延伸至 2028 年。 
持續擴充產能：3Q2Q 水冷快接頭產能利用率達 80%~90%，而新產能目前已經
順利開出，產能由先前的3KK 擴增至 4.5KK，預期 2027 年仍將有擴產規劃；滑軌
產能目前維持在先前規劃的 350K 套的水準 ， 後續將視專案認證進度動態規劃產能
擴充。 
研究部預估 2026 年營收 174 億元(+40%YoY)，毛利率 35.8%，稅後 EPS 63.51
元。(前次預估 2026 稅後 EPS 61.86 元)。 預估2027 年營收 245 億元(+41%YoY)，

群聯(8299)法說會前財報預估 
受惠 AI 帶動儲存需求，4Q26 營運動能延續

| 財報訊息   
群聯即將於 11/6(五)舉行法說會， 3Q26營收季增 26.79%至860.7億
元， 優於預期 ，市場普遍預估毛利率介於56~65%，EPS約124.22元，
關注公司對於2027年NAND市況、產業供需看法以及AI生態系佈局
與應用。 
| 營運現況  
3Q26營收季增26.79%，優於預期：3Q26營收季增26.79%至860.7億
元，優於預期，主要反映AI基礎建設、AI推論及企業級儲存需求快
速成長，2Q26公司AI Ecosystem modules 占比38%，然而9月公司AI 
Ecosystem modules 營收貢獻度已超過整體營收50%，考量在NAND
成本推升下，推估毛利率64.5%，稅後EPS 134.52元。 
| 營運展望  
預估4Q26營收季增 11.92%：預估 4Q26營收季增 11.92%至963.3億
元，動能來自持續受惠AI帶動儲存、運算、網路及相關基礎建設需
求，9月公司AI Ecosystem modules包括 eSSD 、aiDAPTIV solution、
AI PC、AI Networking、AI Server及Boot Drive開機碟等AI相關應用，
營收貢獻度已超過整體營收50%，反映公司Phison 3.0策略轉型，優
化產品結構 ， 目前公司近9成在於高附加價值市場應用 ， 跳脫以往傳
統零售業務景氣波動影響。先前法說公司提及目前 NAND供應短缺
仍是最大限制，預期2H26價格仍維持漲勢，考量隨著購入NAND成
本墊高下，推估毛利率62.4%，稅後EPS 142.08元。展望2027年，隨
著AI推理從文本、圖像、影片走向AI Agent產生TB級資料量，將推
升高容量、高效能儲存需求，將支撐公司後續營運動能。

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
좋은 실적이 상수인 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다. 이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 
이전 전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 26 년 3분기 
영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 상회한 
수준이다. 
실적이 끌고, 수급이 밀고 
지금까지는 높은 실적에 대한 불안보다는 먼 미래에 대한 불확실성을 앞서 주가에 
반영시키는 힘이 더 강하게 작용하다가, 주가가 1,190,000원을 찍은 이후 상승세를 
보이고 있다. 실적을 발표한 10월 28일도 10% 넘게 상승했다. 기대 이상의 실적과 
미국 반도체 시장 분위기가 좋았던 영향이다. 앞으로는 악재에 대해서 민감도가 떨
어지는 구간에 진입할 것으로 전망한다. 지나치게 하락한 주가와 밸류에이션이 버팀
목이 될 것이고, Anthropic의 IPO까지 발표되는 Big Tech 기업들의 실적은 시장의 
우려를 충분히 상쇄할 수 있을 것으로 기대한다. 추가로 진행되는 자사주 매수도 불
안한 수급을 해소할 수 있을 전망이다. 
목표주가는 괴리율을 고려해서 하향 조정 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 26년에도 AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 
시장은 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 
때문이다. 26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 
판단한다. 목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 
업황에 대한 우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 27년 EPS 
538,187원에 PER 6배를 적용하였다.

 SK하이닉스 (000660)

SK하이닉스 (000660)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기, 모든 악재를 잠재운 실적 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다.  이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 이전 
전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 
DRAM 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 35.4% 증가했다. B/G 는 +11.5%, ASP 는 
+28.2%로 추정했다. 3분기 DRAM 가격 상승률은 경쟁사 대비 높은 수준이다. Server 
DRAM 비중이 2분기 대비 높아진 것과 고객 믹스가 개선된 영향으로 추정한다. 
HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정한다. HBM이 3분기 가격에 미친 영향은 
제한적인 수준으로 판단한다.  
NAND 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 18.2% 증가했다. B/G 는 -1%, ASP는 +26%로 
추정한다. 이전 전망치를 상회했다. SSD 비중은 2분기 75%에서 3분기에는 77%로 
상승했다.  
26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 
상회한 수준이다. DRAM 영업이익은 지난 분기 대비 42.3% 증가한 65.1조원으로 
예상한다. 영업이익률은 전 분기 대비 4.0%p 상승한 83.0%로 추정한다. NAND 
영업이익은 전 분기 대비 23.1% 증가한 18.2조원으로 추정한다. 영업이익률은 전 
분기 대비 2.9%p 높아진 72.4%로 추정한다.

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기, DRAM 물량 효과 
SK하이닉스의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 29.4% 증가한 135.3조원으로 
예상한다. 가격상승은 지속될 것으로 예상하지만 26년 3분기 대비 상승폭이 낮아질 
전망이다. 물량은 3분기 대비 25% 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 신규 라인 가동 영향이다. 
4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 34.1% 증가한 111.7조원으로 예상한다. DRAM 
90.9조원, NAND 20.7조원이다.  
DRAM 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 36.4% 증가한 107조원으로 예상한다. ASP 는 
+15%, Bit Growth는 +25.0%로 가정하였다. HBM 출하는 지난 분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 예상하고, HBM4 비중은 60%대로 높아질 전망이다. 
26년 4분기 NAND 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 11.6% 증가할 것으로 예상한다. Bit 
Growth는 +5%로 전망하고, ASP는 +12%로 예상한다. SSD 비중은 소폭 개선될 
전망이다. SSD 내에서 eSSD 비중은 90% 수준이다.

IBKS RESEARCH │5 
공정 전환 속도는 예상보다 빠르게 진행 중: 1cb nm, 321L 
SK하이닉스는 1c nm 비중을 빠르게 확대하고 있다. Wafer 투입이 제한된 상황에서 
공급 확대를 위한 최선의 방법이기 때문이다. HBM 수요는 1b nm를 중심으로 대응할 
예정이다. 이와 관련된 물량도 꾸준히 증가하지만, 출하 Bit 기준으로 1c nm 비중이 
26년 1분기 3% 수준에서 26년 3분기에는 20%, 연말에는 33%까지 높아질 전망이다. 
HBM이 아닌 DRAM 물량이 크게 증가할 전망이다. 이를 통해서 높아진 DRAM 수요 
대응할 것으로 예상한다. 26년 출하 Bit Growth는 28.5%로 상향되었다. 
NAND 생산라인의 공정 전환은 High-End로 꾸준히 변화 중이다. 321L 는 26년 
2분기에는 176L와 Cross over 되었다. 예상보다 1분기 빠른 속도다. 26년 3분기 321L 
비중은 50%이다. 26년말까지 321L를 64%까지 확대할 계획이다.

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
Check Point 
1. DRAM ASP의 차별화 
26년 3분기 DRAM ASP는 메이커별로 차별화가 크다. 26년 2분기 대비 상승폭은 마
이크론이 10% 후반, 삼성전자는 20% 초반으로 추정하고, SK하이닉스는 20% 후반이
다. LTA, HBM 등 다양한 변수가 있지만 SK하이닉스는 고객 비중 변화가 가장 큰 변
수라고 설명한다.  
2. HBM4 물량, 수율 문제 
26년 3분기에 가장 시끄러웠던 변수가 SK하이닉스의 HBM4 관련 품질 문제이다. 26
년 3분기 DRAM 매출과 영업이익으로는 문제가 있었다고 판단하기 어렵다. 26년 3분
기 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정되고 4분기 말에는 비중이 60%
까지 높아질 것으로 전망한다.   
3. HBM 8Hi 확대가 시장 규모 감소는 아니다. 
HBM4는 12Hi가 원래 제품 사양이다. HBM 공급이 부족해지면서 GPU를 좀 더 많이 
팔기 위해서 적용되는 HBM 용량을 줄이는 방향으로 제품 설계가 변경되는 중이다. 
2027년 하반기에는 8Hi 비중이 12Hi 비중을 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 하지만 이로 
인해서 HBM 전체 물량이 감소하는 것은 아닌 것으로 전망하고 있다.  
4. 투자와 생산은 다르다 
SK하이닉스는 용인팹을 공기를 앞당겨서 완공할 계획이다. 이로 인해서 공급량이 예
상 대비 커지는 것에 대한 우려가 있지만 건물 완공과 장비 반입에는 시차가 있고, 수
요를 고려하지 않은 장비 반입은 없을 것으로 밝혔다. 
5. 주주환원. 더 남아 있다 
공시를 통해서 26년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원의 주주환원 계획을 밝혔다. 배당을 30
조원 이상, 자사주 매입/소각을 60조원이상을 추진할 계획이다. 26년 4분기 중에 배
당은 14조원을 집행하고, 자사주는 20조원을 매입할 예정이다. 자사주 20조원 중에서 
10조원은 종업원 상여금 지급용이어서 주주환원에 포함되지 않는다.  
26년 4분기 실적 발표에서 추가적인 배당과 자사주 매입에 대한 계획을 발표할 것으
로 예상한다.

ASP 움직임은 메이커별로 
차별화

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
펀더멘탈과 센티멘탈의 줄다리기 구간 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 시장은 
성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 
26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 판단한다.  
목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 업황에 대한 
우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 2027년 EPS 538,187원에 PER 
6배를 적용하였다.  
아직 제대로 보급되지 않은 Agent AI가 확산되면 컴퓨팅 파워 필요성에 대한 인식도 
크게 바뀔 것으로 전망한다. 메타의 MUSE는 가입자당 8GB의 DRAM을 가정하고 
있다. Open AI, Google, Anthropic 에서도 Agent Platform이 공개된다면 필요한 
DRAM은 폭발적으로 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 
메모리 수요에 대한 규모가 너무 커지는 것이 걱정이지 누가 살 것인지에 대한 것은 
걱정할 문제가 아니라 판단한다. 기존 AI 모델의 진화, 효율적 모델의 등장, Agent AI 
모델의 확대 모두 메모리 수요를 기하급수적으로 확장시키는 변수이다. 
투자 주체들의 자금난이 수요를 저해하는 변수는 아닌 것으로 판단한다. 대체 
투자자들은 지속적으로 증가할 것으로 전망하기 때문이다.  
메모리 업체들의 투자 계획은 수요에 대응하기 위한 투자일 뿐 공급과잉을 만들 
수준은 아닐 것으로 판단한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

주가 추이

3분기 영업 이 익 은 지상방산 매출 감소와 일 회성 비용 등의 영 향 으로 
기대치를 대폭 하회했습니다. 4분기 실적 회복 과 중 장기 실적 성장 대
비 저평가된 밸 류 에이 션 에 주목합 니 다. 
3Q26 Review: 영업이익 0.5조원으로 컨센서스 대폭 하회 
3분기 매출액은 6.1조원으로 전년대비 6.0% 감소했다. 항공우주 부문
과 연결자회사들 성장에도 불구하고 지상방산 매출이 전년대비 약 0.9
조원 감소한 영향이다. 지상방산 내수와 수출 매출은 각각 0.7조원, 0.6
조원으로 전년대비 28.6%, 50.4% 감소했다. 내수는 대전 사업장 생산 
중단, 수출은 4분기 납기 집중에 기인한다. 항공우주 매출은 군수사업 
물량 증가로 전년대비 9.3% 증가한 0.7조원을 기록했다. 연결 자회사
인 한화오션과 한화시스템 매출은 각각 3.4조원, 0.8조원으로 전년대비 
12.9%, 30.1% 증가했다. 영업이익은 0.5조원으로 전년대비 49.2% 감
소했으며 영업이익률은 7.4%로 6.3%P 하락했다. 한화오션과 한화시스
템의 이익 증가에도 지상방산 이익 감소와 필리조선소 적자 반영이 영
향을 미쳤다. 지상방산은 일회성 비용 약 460억원, 원화 강세, 매출 감
소에 따른 고정비 부담 증가가 복합적으로 작용했다. 세전이익은 한화
오션의 외화환산손실 영향으로 -0.2조원을 기록하며 적자전환했다.  
실적 회복과 저평가 매력에 주목할 시점 
4분기 영업이익은 1.7조원으로 추정하며 시장 기대치를 상회할 전망이
다. 지상방산 수출 인도 물량이 집중되는 영향이다. 2027년 예상 실적 
기준 P/E는 12.5배로 경쟁사 Rheinmetall(22.5배) 대비 낮다. 약 35조
원의 방산 수주잔고를 기반으로 최소 2029년까지 실적 성장이 예상되
는 만큼 저평가 매력이 높다. 신규 수주 시점과 실적에 대한 우려가 상
존하지만 현재 밸류에이션에는 상당 부분 반영된 것으로 판단한다. 미
국 중간선거 등 국방예산 관련 정책 불확실성과 환율 부담이 완화되면 
투자자들의 관심은 4분기 실적과 2027년 신규 수주로 이동할 전망이
다. 연말 주요국의 국방예산 확정은 신규 수주 시점에 대한 불확실성을 
낮추는 계기가 될 수 있다.  
투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 1,300,000원 유지 
글로벌 재무장 수요 확대에 따른 동사의 중장기 성장 전망은 여전히 
유효하다. 투자의견 BUY와 목표주가 1,300,000원을 유지한다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 30 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (채운샘) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
한화에어로스페이스는 당사와 ‘독점규제 및 공정거래에 관한 법률’ 에 따른 계열회사 관계에 있는 법인입니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

한화에어로스페이스 (012450) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

유틸리티업종

◼ 목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적, 소규모 SMR 수주 등은 긍정적 
◼ 구체적인 미국 원전 사업개발 시작, 2H27 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
◼ 2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조

목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적 양호, 소규모 SMR 수주는 긍정적 
투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 85,000원으로 하향. 이제는 성장 스토리보다
는 실질적인 원전 수주 확대가 필요한 상황. 의미 있는 수준의 원전 수주 확
대는 2H27부터 본격화될 가능성 존재. 다만, 단기적인 원전 수주 모멘텀 부
족 등을 감안해 6개월 목표주가를 현실화했음 
1) 3Q26 연결기준 영업이익 2,612억원(+91%, 이하 YoY), 에너빌리티 부문 
영업이익 1,211억원(+179%) 등 양호한 실적 기록. 이는 에너빌리티의 가스
터빈, 원전 기자재 매출 증가 등에 기인, 2) 10월 29일 NuScale과 1개 모듈 
주기기 공급 계약을 체결했으며, 4Q26 X-energy와 4개 모듈의 일부 장납기 
품목/소재 수주가 예상되는 등 2Q26 ~ 3Q26 TerraPower와의 일부 품목 계
약에 이어 개별 건의 규모는 작지만 다수의 SMR 관련 기자재 수주가 늘어나
는 점은 긍정적. 3) 최근 폴란드 AP1000 3기의 선발주 장납기 품목 확정(폴
란드 공급업체의 대상 생산 품목 확정, 원자로 압력용기 및 증기발생기는 미
확정)되었으며, 미국 원전 프로젝트가 구체화되기 시작. 4) 향후 AP1000향 
공급 기자재 품목 확대 가능성도 존재. 이는 2H27 본격적인 원전 수주금액 
확대로 이어질 것으로 기대

미국 원전은 구체적 사업개발 시작, 2H27 이후 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
2026년 6월 DOE의 ‘원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 발표(AP1000 10기)’, 8월 
TVA의 ‘2026 IRP’에 2040년까지 최대 원전 5.4GW 승인, 9월 한국과 미국 
상무부 간의 원전 투자 8기 MOU 체결 등을 통해 미국 원전 건설 재개 정책 
및 제도 개편이 구체화되고 강화되었음. 미국 원전 시장은 4Q26 ~ 2027년 
구체적인 사업개발 단계를 거쳐(부지 선정, 원전 운영 SPC 설립, 금융계약체
결, COL 신청 등), 2H27 ~ 2028년 장납기품목 선발주, 2028 ~ 2029년 건
설 계약 단계 진입 예상. 동사는 4Q26 미국 및 국내 가스터빈, 국내 공공 해
상풍력 프로젝트용 터빈, 폴란드 AP1000 주기기 등 미국 원전 이외 분야에
서의 수주 확대 예상. 현재 Rolls-Royce 등과도 SMR 주기기 공급 관련 논의 
중이며, NuScale SMR은 2027년 PPA 체결 시, COL 및 FID 이전 SMR 모듈 
11기(4Q26 1기 계약)의 수주가 가능할 것으로 기대

2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조 
대우건설·두산에너빌리티 컨소시엄의 체코 원전 시공 계약 협상은 종료되었
지만, 2027년 7월 신규 시공사와 계약 예정. 동사는 입찰에 다시 참여할 예
정. 이번 이슈의 핵심 쟁점은 이미 체코 측과 공사비총액(Lump-sum) 계약을 
맺은 한수원이 시공사와 총액계약을 맺기를 원한 것임. 이번 이슈로 체코 두
코바니 원전 EPC 프로젝트 수익성에 대한 우려가 발생할 수 있지만, 실제 건
설 과정에서 추가 비용이 공사비 계약금액(예비비 포함)을 초과할지는 예단하
기 어려움. 하도급 관리, 공기 준수 등 시공능력의 중요성이 강조되는 상황. 
동사는 3년 전부터 체코에 시공 인력 상주하며 시공 환경 자료 확보, 초기 인
허가, 설계 업무 관여해 왔으며, 주기기 공급사로서 기자재 특성을 활용해 설
치까지 수행 가능한 경쟁력 등을 바탕으로 성공적 수주를 목표하고 있음

KOSPI 5937.66 
시가총액 36,320십억원 
시가총액비중 0.55% 
자본금(보통주) 3,267십억원 
52주 최고/최저 136,400원 / 56,700원 
120일 평균거래대금 2,686억원

 미 상무부 전략적 협력사업 최대 10기 최대 11.5GW 정부 협력사업(한국은 6기 투자/건설) 
 V.C. Summer 2·3호기 공사 재개 2기 2.3GW 장납기 품목 발주·EPC(LLI/EPC) 
폴란드 Lubiatowo-Kopalino 3기 3.5GW 장납기 품목 발주·EPC(LLI/EPC)

우크라이나 Khmelnytskyi 5·6호기 2기 2.3GW 초기 서비스 계약(ESC) 
정부 지원·협력 및 계약 기반 사업 소계  최대 29기 최대 33.4GW FEED·초기 사업발굴 제외

지원 대상 최대 5개 프로젝트, 프로젝트당 AP1000 2기, 합계 10기 
프로젝트 소유 구조 Westinghouse와 해당 전력회사 또는 에너지기업이 공동 소유 
대출 접근 전 자기자본 요건 프로젝트당 Westinghouse 5억 달러, 상대 전력회사·에너지기업 5억 달러, 합계 10억 달러를 사전에 확약 
조달 방식 Westinghouse가 참여 기업과 함께 장납기품목을 고정가격으로 조달 
조달 시점 자기자본 확약 시점과 기타 조건에 따라 프로젝트별 구매를 순차적으로 진행

표 4. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업의 대상 장납기품목(LLI; Long-Lead Items) 
 품목 설명  대출과 관련된 확인 근거 
원자로압력용기 
(RPV; Reactor Pressure Vessel) 
핵연료와 노심을 수용하고 원자로 냉각재의 압력을 유지하는 
용기. 앞서 설명한 격납용기와 구분되는 원자로 주기기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
증기발생기 
(SG; Steam Generator) 
원자로에서 나온 열을 전달받아 터빈을 돌릴 증기를 만드는 대
형 열교환기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
사전제작 구조 모듈 
(PSM; Prefabricated Structural Modules) 
원전 건물의 벽체·바닥 등 구조물을 모듈로 미리 제작하는 방
식의 구성품. AP1000에는 강판과 콘크리트로 구성하는 구조 
모듈이 사용됨 
DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23. 다만, 미국 대
출자료에 개별 모듈 코드까지 열거되지는 않음 
원자로냉각재펌프 
(RCP; Reactor Coolant Pump) 
원자로 냉각재를 순환시켜 원자로에서 발생한 열을 증기발생
기로 전달하는 펌프 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
격납용기 
(CV; Containment Vessel) 
원자로압력용기와 주요 설비를 둘러싸며 방사성 물질의 외부 
방출을 억제하는 대형 강철 밀폐 구조물 
DOE EDF 담당자의 직접 발언에서 확인 — 
2026.10.20 공개 인터뷰 
자료: DOE, 대신증권 Research Center

(26.10.21) 
1,417,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회 
◼ 4Q26 지연 물량 인식 및 신규 공장 기여로 실적 개선 전망 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용). 기
존 공장의 높은 가동률을 기반으로 5공장 매출 기여 확대, 미국 생산거점 
확보, 신규 모달리티 진출을 통한 중장기 성장 지속 전망.

3Q26 Review: 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회  
3Q26 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5% yoy, +12.8% qoq), 영업이익 6,401
억원(+1.1% yoy, +9.1% qoq), 영업이익률 42.9%(-7.5%p yoy)를 기록하며 
당사 추정치 및 시장 컨센서스 상회. 5월 1차 파업에 따른 매출 차질(약 
1,500억원) 에도 불구하고, 기존 1~4공장 Full 가동이 지속되며 견조한 실
적 달성. 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대와 및 미국 록빌 공장 매출 인식도 외
형 성장에 기여. 다만, 신규 생산시설 초기 가동비와 제품 믹스 영향으로 
영업이익률은 전년 동기 대비 하락.

4Q26 매출액 1조 5,764억원(+22.6% yoy), 영업이익 7,075억원
(+34.0% yoy) 전망  
4Q26에는 2분기 파업으로 지연된 생산물량의 순차적인 매출 인식과 미국 
록빌 공장 매출 기여, 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대로 실적 개선 예상, 동사
는 신규 생산시설의 매출 가시성 확보를 바탕으로 2026년 매출 성장률 
20%를 무난히 달성할 것으로 전망(연초 가이던스 15~20%).

신규 수주 회복과 포트폴리오 확장에 주목 
미국 의약품 관세정책 불확실성 완화로 지연됐던 수주 협상이 재개되며 신
규 수주 환경 개선 중. 3Q26 약 3억달러의 신규 수주를 확보했으며, 대형 
계약 협상도 구체화되고 있어 2027년 수주 회복 기대. 이는 5공장 가동률 
확대와 중장기 성장 가시성을 높이는 요인. 6공장은 ‘26년말~’27년초 착
공을 목표로 기존 증설 일정 유지. 
폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 1차 공개매수 응모율 87.88%를 기록하며 연내 
인수 완료 추진 중. 인수 완료 시 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업영역이 확대되며 
글로벌 생산거점 및 고객 기반 다변화 기대.

- Neutral(중립) 
  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률과 유사한 수준 예상

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 
2026-10-14

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 820,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 366,500 원 
상승여력: 123.7%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 및 환율 영향으로 시장 예상치 하회 예상 
Key: 4Q26부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 계약 수주 모멘텀 기대 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 4 행정 엔진&FDC으로 주가 상승여력 
충분. 매수

3Q26 Preview: 컨센서스 하회하는 실적 기록 예상 
3Q26 연결 매출액 6조 464억원(+36.9% YoY, -4.5% QoQ), 영업이익 9,230억
원(+65.6% YoY, -11.2% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(1조 70억원)를 하회하는 실적 기
록할 것으로 추정한다. 이전 분기 대비 중대재해 및 하계휴가, 그리고 추석 연휴에 따른 
조업일수 감소 효과와 더불어 큰 폭의 환율 하락 영향(약 500억원) 때문이다. 홍범도
함 화재 및 임단협 관련 일회성 비용은 이번 분기가 아닌 4Q 에 반영될 것으로 예상한
다. 해양 부문에서 트리온 FPU 프로젝트 C/O에 따른 일부 환입 발생한 것으로 추정
되며, 해양 도크에서 컨테이너선 선체 블록 일부 건조로 고정비 감소효과 일부 상쇄 중
이다. 다만 올해 연내 혹은 내년 초 추가 해양 수주 없을 시 고정비 상승 우려 확대될 
전망이다. 동사 올해 상선 수주 목표(114.7억불)를 이미 초과하는 수주 실적 기록한 상
황이며, 고마진 선종인 LNGC 및 VLGC/VLAC 중심으로 수주했기 때문에 ‘28~’29
년 구간 경쟁사 대비 뚜렷한 선종 믹스 효과를 기대한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 82 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 97 만원에서 82
만원으로 하향한다. 중국의 공격적인 캐파 증설 지속과 더불어 중국 대형 조선사들의 
글로벌 선주가 발주한 LNGC 물량 및 원가 경쟁력 확대에 따른 상선 부문의 멀티플 
조정이 불가피하다는 판단이다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클
과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지
속되고 있는 중으로 파악된다. 올해 4 분기부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 부문의 본격적
인 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 전망이며, 해당 모멘텀에 따른 주가 상승
여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)를 유지한다.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
CONSUMER PANEL Q3 2026 
MORE RESILIENCE
3 November 2026
• Average consumer spend across 
the Westpac-DataX Consumer 
Panel remained resilient, rising 
1.5% in Q3 and 4.6%yr. 
• While some of this reflected a price-
driven surge in fuel spend (+0.3%qtr) 
discretionary categories also posted 
robust gains (contributing 0.9ppts 
to the headline rise) with non-fuel 
essential categories contributing 
positively as well (+0.5ptps).
• Households continued to add to savings 
and buffers. Savings rose 1.3%qtr, led 
by younger households and mortgagors, 
with average savings buffers increasing 
to 22.2 months of mortgage repayments. 
• The detail shows financial pressures 
remain uneven but spending patterns 
appear to be converging across 
consumers with low, medium and high 
buffers. Much of this appears to relate 
to a reduction in fuel consumption.

Westpac–DataX Consumer Panel
The Westpac–DataX Consumer Panel is a large 
dataset that gives a timely and detailed picture 
of Australian consumer finances and behaviour. 
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics 
team, the dataset links transaction activity with 
balance sheet information to give a complete view 
of income, spending, saving and borrowing flows. 
All data is de-identified and aggregated to 
ensure privacy. The resulting sample of over 
one million customers gives an accurate 
representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer and is perfectly suited 
to quickly tracing responses to events like 
interest rate moves and energy price shocks.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Heatmap of monthly spending categories
The Westpac DataX Consumer Panel  shows average
consumer spending remained resilient in Q3, rising 1.5%
despite a further lift in fuel costs. Fuel contributed 0.3ppts
to quarterly growth, but discretionary spending did more

of the heavy lifting, contributing 0.9ppts as recreation
and travel-related spending recovered. Households also
continued to add to their financial buffers: savings up 1.3%
in the quarter and average buffers across households with
a mortgage increasing to 22.2 months of repayments.
Beneath the aggregate picture, lower-buffer households
remain more exposed to higher fuel costs and mortgage
repayments, but their discretionary spending share is
looking increasingly similar to other households.

Spending holds up despite higher fuel costs
Average household spending increased 1.5% in Q3, with much
of the strength again driven by transport-related spending.
Fuel contributed 0.3ppts to quarterly growth, while transport
excluding fuel contributed a further 0.4ppts. Excluding the
direct effects of the price-driven lift in fuel, average spend
was up 1.2%qtr. Importantly, stronger fuel spending did not
come at the expense of discretionary purchases. Discretionary

categories contributed 0.9ppts to quarterly spending growth,
compared with 0.5ppts from non-fuel essentials.
Solid rise in non-fuel spend and financial buffers
Discretionary spending dominates
Recreation and culture contributed 0.3ppts to growth, reflecting
stronger spending on sporting services, while restaurants and
hotels added a further 0.2ppts. Transport spending was also
boosted by a rebound in airline expenditure, which more than
unwound the weakness recorded in Q2 when refunds relating

to cancellations due to conflict in the Middle East weighed on
the category. Discretionary spending rose 0.9%qtr, following a
flat result and a 0.1% rise in the previous two quarters.

3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Savings growth by mortgage status
Households continue to rebuild savings
Savings increased 1.3% in Q3, with the strongest gains again 
recorded among younger households. Savings balances for 
18–34 year olds increased 2.0%qtr, compared with 0.7% 
for those aged 55 and over. Mortgagors also continued to 
accumulate savings more rapidly, recording a 2.8% increase 
compared with 0.7% among non-mortgagors. Savings growth 
among mortgagors has now outpaced non-mortgagors for 
three consecutive years.
Stronger savings have continued to lift mortgage buffers. 
Average buffers (savings divided by the sum of essential 
expenses and minimum mortgage repayments), increased to 
22.2 months of repayments in September. Looking within the 
unit-record sample, median mortgage buffers jumped from 
6.3 months in August to 6.8 months, its highest level since the 
series began in 2019.

Median mortgage buffers
Fuel spending a clear drag on consumers
Fuel spending as a share of total spending
Spending outcomes continued to vary across age groups. 
The squeeze remains most apparent among younger 
households. Fuel spending rose 5.1%qtr for 18–34 year olds, 
against a 1.5%qtr increase in non-fuel spending. By contrast, 
non-fuel spending increased 2.1%qtr among households aged 
55 and over. 
The gap between mortgagors and non-mortgagors was 
more modest. Spending rose 1.6%qtr among mortgagors 
and 1.9%qtr among those without a mortgage, suggesting 
mortgage status alone masks considerable variation in 
household financial positions – a divide that becomes clearer 
when households are grouped by the size of their mortgage 
buffers (see discussion further on in this note). Note that ‘non 
mortgagors’ covers a diverse range of consumers including 
those that are renting and those that own their homes without 
a mortgage.

### Page 4

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
4
WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Median litres of fuel consumed
More importantly, their consumption appears to be less 
responsive to price movements. During periods of cheaper 
fuel, high-buffer households show a noticeably larger lift in 
litres purchased, consistent with stockpiling when prices are 
favourable. Low-buffer households show much less of this 
response, while their fuel volumes have also adjusted less 
during the recent increase in fuel costs.
The spending data show the consequence. Fuel expenditure 
has risen sharply across all three groups recently, but remains 
highest among low-buffer households, where it also absorbs 
a larger share of total spending. Food spending has also 
declined since 2023. This may partly reflect households 
economising within the category, including switching towards 
cheaper or home-brand products, although further work is 
needed to test this.
Taken together, the households with the smallest savings 
cushions face higher mortgage repayments, spend more 
on fuel and appear to have less scope to adjust their fuel 
consumption when prices change. 
Discretionary spend as a share of total
This suggests that in aggregate, household balance sheets 
are looking healthy. The picture is a little at odds with the very 
weak reads on our Westpac-Melbourne Index Consumer 
Sentiment Index which suggests finances are coming under 
much more pressure. The fact that both average spending and 
saving both rose suggests incomes over the quarter were not 
overly stretched and that more of the weakness in sentiment 
may relate to concerns about the outlook (something that may 
also be encouraging consumers to build buffers now, while they 
still can).
Pressures intense for low-buffer households
Aggregate buffer measures mask sizeable differences across 
mortgagors. We can get a sense of these variations by 
combining mortgagors into three broad groups according to 
buffer size, i.e. low, middle and high buffer groups. This shows 
that households with the smallest buffers are also more 
exposed to both interest rates and fuel costs.

The buffer differences are relatively stark, the median savings 
of the low-buffer group just $2k compared to $15k for the 
middle-buffer group and $95k for the high buffer group. 
Low buffer households face median minimum mortgage 
repayments of around $2,600 a month, compared with $2,400 
for the middle buffer group and $2,200 for the highest-buffer 
group. 
Low buffer households also spend more on fuel, at around 
$170 a month compared with $160 for the middle buffer 
group and $150 for the high buffer group.

We can use average pump prices to estimate what this 
expenditure implies for fuel volumes. Low-buffer households 
consume around 70 litres a month, middle-buffer households 
67 litres a month and the high-buffer group around 62 litres. 
This compares to an average of 81, 80 and 70 respectively 
through 2024-25. 
Median fuel spend by customer

### Page 5

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts are based are reasonable, 
the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Yet despite this greater exposure, the gap in broader spending 
behaviour has narrowed. High-buffer households continue 
to spend more on discretionary categories, averaging around 
$1,940 a month compared with $1,740 for low-buffer 
households. However, their discretionary share has been 
broadly stable since 2023, while the shares of low- and 
middle-buffer households have risen by close to a percentage 
point bringing them more in line with the high-buffer group.

Some of the convergence reflects a longer run down-trend in 
fuel spending that has been more pronounced amongst low 
and middle buffer households. This lowering in fuel spend lifts 
the share available for other categories.

Essential spending excluding fuel, meanwhile, has changed 
little across the three cohorts since 2023. The shift in 
spending patterns has largely occurred on the discretionary 
side.

The result is a somewhat mixed picture. Low-buffer 
households remain considerably more exposed to mortgage 
and fuel costs and have far smaller savings cushions. But their 
discretionary spending has nevertheless moved closer to that 
of better-buffered households, suggesting some recovery in 
spending flexibility despite these constraints. 
Conclusion
Aggregate household finances remain resilient: spending and 
savings both increased in Q3 and mortgage buffers continued 
to rebuild. Discretionary spending has also held up despite the 
sharp increase in fuel costs.

But aggregate resilience masks a much more uneven 
distribution of pressure. Low-buffer households 
simultaneously face higher mortgage repayments, greater fuel 
exposure and much smaller savings cushions. While these 
households have so far maintained discretionary spending, 
their capacity to absorb further pressure is clearly more 
limited.
About the Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics service for 
institutional and business banking customers, the dataset links 
transaction activity with balance sheet information to give 
a complete view of income, spending, saving and borrowing 
flows. 
The resulting sample of over one million customers provides 
an accurate representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer. Monthly observations are available back 
to January 2019 with timely updates provided a few weeks 
after the end of each month. As such, it is perfectly suited to 
quickly tracing the impact and responses to events like interest 
rate moves and energy price shocks.

Document 314: Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation

Source PDF: all report pdf/Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

Downgrade of global 
equities to a Core allocation  
Summary 
Event: We are downgrading global equities from Overweight 
to a Neutral/Core allocation. We continue to maintain a 
Neutral allocation to fixed income, Overweight on Gold and 
Underweight on Cash. There are more details in our Global 
Market Outlook publication on 30 October 2026. 
What does the equity downgrade mean?  
This downgrade implies a slightly lower allocation to global 
equities within a diversified portfolio. For our CIO Foundation 
Balanced portfolio, this would imply an equity allocation of 
52.5%, down from 53 .2%. We continue to expect positive 
returns for global equities  in the coming 6 -12 months , 
though equity investors may need to recalibrate expectations 
lower, compared with the 15 -20% annualised gains 
experienced in the last few years. Our capital market 
assumptions still suggest a mid -to-high single digit annual 
returns over the next few years, consistent with a transition 
to a mid -cycle bull market . Consensus estimates point to 
16% earnings growth for global equities in 2027, which 
should overcome various headwinds to deliver positive 
returns for equities over the next 12 months. Investors should 
thus, continue to maintain exposure to equities  within a 
diversified portfolio. 
In addition, we see a changing playbook  to prepare for 
higher volatility, more active management and greater 
diversification, as discussed below. 
What does it mean for regional equities 
positioning? 
Within a global equities portfolio, we remain Overweight US 
and Asia ex -Japan (AxJ) equities , which are expected to 
deliver the highest earnings growth in 2027 amongst the 
major regions. This growth is led by the technology sector, 
which carries the largest weight in both regions. Within the 
diversified equities portfolio, we maintain Core allocations to

53.2% for our CIO Foundation Balanced portfolio

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

Euro area equities, which offer lower valuations, and Japan 
equities, which is backed by a reflating economy. We are 
Underweight UK equities, where the sector composition 
offers lower growth prospects in 2027. Within the AxJ region, 
we are Overweight Taiwan equities, with attractive structural 
growth driven by its semiconductor industry. We are also 
Overweight India equities, which offers diversified non-tech 
growth drivers. Taiwan and India equities both generate 
attractive returns on equity. We maintain Core allocations to 
China equities, with low valuations, and Korea equities, with 
explosive earnings growth in its semiconductors industry, but 
volatile price movements. We are Underweight ASEAN, 
which offers relatively lower earnings growth. 
What are the implications for equity sectors? 
We are Overweight these sectors globally: US 
information technology, US healthcare, US financials, US 
materials, Europe ex -UK financials, and Japan 
financials. 
A framework we use is to break the market into four key 
sector building blocks. Tech is the largest block, 
encompassing the Information Technology, Communication 
Services and Consumer Discretionary sectors. Put 
differently, the segment spans five major industries: 
semiconductors, hardware, internet, software, and IT 
services. Within the US market, we expect this segment to 
deliver c.30% earnings growth in 2027, extending the strong 
momentum seen in 2026. 
Financials and Real Estate  are the next building block, 
comprising these two sectors. We expect this segment to 
continue delivering low -teens earnings growth in 2027, 
supported by resilient economic activity and healthy credit 
trends. 
Resources include companies across four sectors: 
Industrials, Materials, Energy, and Utilities . We expect the 
segment to generate 10 -15% earnings growth in 2027, 
although Energy is likely to remain the largest drag on overall 
growth due to tougher comparisons and softer energy price 
assumptions for 2027. 
Healthcare & Others  consists primarily of Healthcare and 
Consumer Staples. We expect mid-teen earnings growth for 
the segment in 2027, with a rebound in healthcare earnings 
representing a potential upside surprise. 
Our Overweight sector exposures are drawn from each of the 
four key blocks above. Unlike the last few years , with 
concentrated gains from Tech providing market leadership, 
we see our downgrade in global equities also reflecting a 
broadening in market leadership towards each of the other 
sector blocks apart from Tech.

US and Asia ex-Japan would continue to lead in 
earnings growth in 2027

As structural themes such as AI transition from the rapid 
buildout phase towards monetisation, diversified exposure 
across sectors and regions should become increasingly 
important. 
What are the implications for equity 
opportunistic ideas? 
We have opportunistic ideas on Global High -Dividend 
equities, US healthcare, Euro area banks , MSCI 
Switzerland and Japan banks. The selection represents a 
more defensive stance  currently, with an average beta of 
below 1 relative to global equities (MSCI AC World), but 
attractive drivers nonetheless, for each of the ideas.  
Global High-Dividend offers higher-income equity allocation 
with historically lower volatility than global equities. US 
healthcare is supported by wider adoption of obesity 
treatments, advance s in oncology a nd innovative medical 
devices, that support earnings growth. Euro area banks  
continue to see steady earnings growth adding to their 
excess capital, which is being returned to shareholders via 
attractive dividend and share buyback yields. Japan banks 
continue to enjoy net margin expansion from BoJ policy 
normalisation, as well as corporate governance 
improvements. MSCI Switzerland is a newly initiated idea for 
investors seeking defensive exposure. H ealthcare and 
consumer staples account s for more than half the index 
weight, providing exposure to relatively resilient demand that 
is more insulated from economic cycles and AI trends. It also 
has a sizeable financial sector supported by steady wealth 
management growth and well-capitalised insurers with 
disciplined underwriting and resilient premium income. 
We see the case for more active management  in a mid -
cycle transition, as higher market dispersion should create 
greater opportunities for active sector and stock selection, 
particularly as leadership within equities evolves. 
What are the implications of higher equity 
market volatility?  
A more uncertain political backdrop and p otentially maturing 
growth rates in certain market segments may contribute to 
more frequent bouts of market volatility. Higher volatility 
presents an opportunity to use structured strategies to gain 
exposure to equities. Such strategies may be used to 
generate income or to gain exposure at a discounted entry 
price. Investors can also consider strategies that are 
structured to return principal or partial principal at maturity. 
What alternative strategies are suited to a 
regime of moderating equity market returns?  
Investors can consider alternative strategies such as hedge  
fund strategies or private markets, for those with the capacity

to accept some illiquidity.  
Equity long/short hedge fund  strategies would retain growth 
participation with less market exposure. Fund managers buy 
companies they expect to outperform and short those they 
expect to underperform, seeking to earn the difference 
between the two , and benefit even as total equity market 
returns are moderating. 
Within private markets , private equity provides  a means to 
higher returns with the effects of an illiquidity premium. 
Meanwhile, i nfrastructure offers  differentiated exposure to 
structural growth opportunities, where c ontracted long-term 
revenues reduce dependence on equity-market sentiment. 
What are the implications on fixed income 
and FX markets? 
Bonds: This year’s surge in bond yields means bonds now 
offer a hedge against any equity market weakness. We 
believe the 3 -7-year part of the US bond yield curve offers 
the best hedge against any equity sell -off caused by AI -
related growth worries. This part of the bond curve has priced 
in most Fed rate hike expectations, which could reverse if 
growth concerns return. Meanwhile, we favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a hedge against any 
fuel price-led inflation scare.  
Credit: For corporate bonds, adopting a defensive stance in 
a volatile environment typically involves: i) increasing credit 
quality, favouring higher-rated issuers; and ii) moving up the 
capital structure, by reducing exposure to subordinated debt 
and favouring secured debt. From a sector perspective, 
large, diversified banks, utilities, and quasi -sovereigns tend 
to be more defensive, particularly relative to cyclical sectors, 
such as metals and mining, exploration, and technology.         
FX: We favour bearish AUD/JPY as a tactical hedge against 
any equity market correction. Review of 21 past S&P500 
correction sessions (2010 -2025) shows AUD weakened 
against USD in 86% of cases, while JPY strengthened 
against USD in 67%, supporting our bearish AUD/JPY 
outlook during any equity market stress.

Westpac New Zealand Economics
Turning point?
• We expect the unemployment rate to edge
down to 5.5% for the September quarter.
• Job numbers have been gaining momentum
in recent months, and now look to be enough
to outstrip population growth. However, the
participation rate will be the deciding factor.
• Despite cost-of-living pressures on workers,
the existing slack in the labour market is likely
to keep wage growth subdued.
• Our forecasts are a little more positive than

what the RBNZ assumed in their September
policy statement. Assuming they deliver on
an OCR hike this afternoon, the labour market
surveys could have an important bearing on
whether they follow up with another hike in
December.

The New Zealand economy has returned to growth over
the last year, despite a number of headwinds. That’s been
accompanied by some modest growth in jobs, though
to date it hasn’t been enough to prevent a further rise

in the unemployment rate. However, we think that next
Wednesday’s labour market reports could show the jobs
market reaching a turning point.
We expect the unemployment rate to ease from 5.6%
to 5.5% in the September quarter, which would mark
the first fall in almost five years. That’s not a firmly held
view, given the usual margin of uncertainty around this
ECONOMIC BULLETIN
Preview of Q3 labour market surveys:

28 Oct 2026 | Michael Gordon, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5670 | michael.gordon@westpac.co.nz

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
measure. But we’re now seeing consistent evidence
that jobs growth is outstripping the modest pace of
population growth. We also note that the previous result
was 5.56% in unrounded terms, so it wouldn’t take much
to tip the rounded rate down to 5.5% this time.
On the whole, our forecasts are a little stronger than
what the RBNZ assumed in its September Monetary
Policy Statement. While our unemployment rate forecast

is identical, we’re more positive on both employment
and labour force participation. Meanwhile, our pick of a
0.5% rise in the Labour Cost Index is slightly ahead of the
RBNZ’s estimate of 0.4%.
Assuming that the RBNZ delivers the OCR hike that we
expect this afternoon, we think they will be noncommittal
about the chances of another hike at the 9 December
Monetary Policy Statement. In that respect, the labour
market surveys could be important in setting market

expectations for the December review, as there aren’t
that many major data releases between now and then.
Forecast details.
We expect the Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS)
to show a 0.5% rise in employment for the September
quarter. That’s broadly in line with the Monthly
Employment Indicator (MEI), with the September release
this morning showing a third straight month of growth
in filled jobs. We note that employment growth in the
HLFS has actually outstripped the MEI for the last nine

quarters, but it’s not clear why this has been the case and
we’re wary of extrapolating it too far into the next quarter.

Employment growth of around 0.5% would be more than
enough to absorb the modest 0.3% rise in the working-
age population, and on its own would suggest a fall in

the unemployment rate. But it also comes down to the
rate of labour force participation, which we don’t really
have much guidance on. The participation rate has been
choppy over recent quarters, and in the June quarter
we saw an unusually large 0.3pt rise to 70.7%; we’re
assuming some payback this time with a more modest
0.1pt rise to 70.8%.
We’ll also be keeping an eye on the broader indicators of
slack in the labour market, although we don’t formally use

these in our forecasts. The June quarter saw a sharp rise
in the underutilisation rate from 12.9% to 13.8%. However,
we think there’s some residual seasonality in this series,
relating to one component in particular: people who are
available for work but not actively looking. In that case,
we could see a substantial pullback in the underutilisation
measure this time.
Previously we’ve characterised the labour market as a
“low-fire, low-hire” environment, with much of the rise in

unemployment reflecting the difficulty of breaking into
the workforce, rather than job losses. There are some
signs of a thaw emerging, with job vacancies picking up
from their lows over the last year and business surveys
pointing to a lift in hiring intentions. But we still expect
that it will be a long road to recovery – our current
forecasts see the unemployment rate at 5% by the end of
next year.
While cost-of-living pressures are again rearing their

head as a major concern for workers, the existing degree
of slack in the labour market will continue to make it
difficult for them to push for bigger pay increases. We
expect a 0.5% rise in the Labour Cost Index (LCI) for
the September quarter, which would nudge the annual
growth rate up slightly to 2.1%. That said, we don’t have a
lot to go on for this forecast. The MEI includes a measure
of median weekly earnings, which has shown no sign of
acceleration so far – but this is only a very loose indicator

of wage pressures, as it will also reflect any changes in
the composition of jobs and pay rates.

Global Market Outlook 
Transitioning 
to mid-cycle



Where are the tactical 
opportunities?  
Are central banks likely to 
continue hiking rates? 
Are quantitative models 
still bullish on equities?

Higher bond yields are improving the 
outlook for future returns and offering 
increasingly attractive income 
opportunities. We are Overweight 
Emerging Market (EM) USD bonds and 
favour corporate over sovereign debt, but 
limit duration to 3-7 years.

We remain Overweight gold. Prices are 
likely to grind higher as near-term 
headwinds from higher bond yields are 
offset by EM central bank demand.

^New 
Transitioning to mid-cycle 
• We now favour a more balanced stance between stocks and bonds . Near-
term event risks and high  earnings expectations prompt us to trim  equities to a 
Core holding.  The US and Asia ex -Japan (AxJ) remain our preferred regions. 
Within AxJ, we upgrade India to Overweight. 
• Higher bond yields are improving the outlook for future returns and offering 
increasingly attractive  income opportunities. We are Overweight Emerging 
Market (EM) USD bonds and favour corporate over government bonds, but limit 
duration to 3-7 years. 
• We remain  Overweight gold . Prices are likely to grind higher as near -term 
headwinds from higher bond yields are offset by EM central bank demand. 
Great expectations 
Individual risk events, high  earnings expectations and a higher cost of capital 
point to a more moderate return environment ahead.  In the very near term, 
financial markets must navigate key event risks, such as (i) the US mid-term elections, 
which have historically been associated with elevated market volatility and (ii) renewed 
geopolitical tensions, with several reports suggesting the US is considering renewed 
strikes on Iran . These  concerns have pushed the Brent oil price well above USD 
110/bbl despite significant ongoing crude oil flows through the Strait of Hormuz.  
Higher bond yields combined with high earnings expectations raise the risk of 
an expectations reset. The US Q3 earnings season is attracting significant attention 
given high earnings expectations, particularly f or the AI sector. However, higher US 
bond yields may start to bite as debt-financed capex takes on a greater share of total 
capex over the coming year. This, combined with already-high expectations, raises the 
risk of disappointment and a near-term repricing of expected earnings growth pace.  
Despite t he current c hallenges, healthy growth continues to underpin the 
economic backdrop . The US economic outlook remains well supported , and the 
absolute level of year-ahead earnings expectations is high. We do not expect Fed rate 
hikes to result in overly restrictive policy. Our caution, thus, stems from the risk of an 
expectations repricing rather than an outright economic slowdown.

A more balanced stock-bond mix 
We trim global equities to a Core holding, leaving us 
Neutral on both equities and bonds and Overweight gold. 
The concerns laid out earlier point to near-term volatility risks 
and a somewhat lower long -term growth trajectory for 
equities. Our quant model also reflects  this cautious outlook, 
having downgraded equities to a level usually associated with 
flat-to-modestly-negative returns. 
From a relative perspective, though, the higher yields on 
offer from bonds also raise the bar  by presenting an 
increasingly compelling return outlook after several 
years of very low yields. We believe this argues for portfolios 
that are increasingly balanced between equities and bonds. 
Ways to diversify equity exposure 
We continue to favour global equity market 
diversification, with a preference for US and AxJ equities. 
We remain Overweight US and A xJ equities, expecting them 
to outperform other major regions. Earnings growth remains 
the main driver of this view – while growth rates could 
decelerate somewhat, we still expect the m to remain higher 
than those of other major regions.  
Japan and Euro area equities remain attractive as 
regional diversifiers.  Our positive view on both markets’  
financial sectors supports our Core holding view on them.   
Within Asia, we are Overweight Taiwan and India.  Indian 
equities have lagged the region this year, in part due to limited 
listed AI -related exposure. However, we believe this is 
increasingly likely to become an advantage as investors seek 
greater diversification away from excessive concentration in 
the AI theme. This comes at a time when the economic and 
earnings growth outlook for Indian equities is starting to 
improve. We retain a more Neutral view on Chinese equities 
amid a still-challenging growth outlook. 
We also favour greater sector diversification . In the US, 
we favour diversifying across our Overweight sectors of 
technology, financials, materials and healthcare. We also 
favour financials in Japan and the Euro area.  
Changes to our equity opportunistic idea s also reflect a 
more defensive stance. In recent weeks, we have closed our 
global semiconductors, MSCI Taiwan, US communication 
services and global power & electrification ideas and have 
opened new ideas on US healthcare and MSCI Switzerland. 
Riding the yield train 
We believe bond markets offer an attractive opportunity 
to lock in yields. The 10-year US government bond yield is 
now in a range last witnessed in 2007. While the process of 
rising yields results in lower bond prices – as painfully 
experienced in recent months  – the yield level itself starts to 
significantly improve the outlook for future returns. 
Favour corporate over government bonds, but check 
your duration. Still-high credit quality means that we continue 
to favour taking on credit risk to earn a higher yield (EM USD 
bonds are preferred, but we also see investment -grade (IG) 
and high -yield (HY) bonds as Core holdings). However, 
limiting duration to 3 -7 years remains key to avoiding 
excessive exposure to any inflation surprises. 
Changes to our bond opportunistic ideas also reflect a 
more balanced stance.  We close our EU  bank contingent 
convertible bonds (CoCos2 – please refer page 29 ) and US 
utilities corporate hybrids ideas. We also close our 
opportunistic idea on US 10-year government bonds following 
the recent pullback in yields, locking in gains. 
Alternatives and gold to remain relevant 
Alternatives are an effective  tool for portfolio risk 
management. Hedge funds and private markets provide a 
differentiated return stream , while  rotating into alternatives  
helps reduce  equity beta exposure . We examine downside 
capture and how beta is reduced via hedge fund sub -
strategies and private markets in our asset allocation section.  
Gold remains a key Overweight. We trim our three- and 12-
month forecasts modestly, but see gold forming a strong base 
above USD 4 ,000/oz. Rising bond yields and the USD were 
recent headwinds, but we expect a softer USD and continued 
EM central bank demand to result in 12-month gains.

The Foundation and Foundation+ models are allocations that you can use as the starting point for building a diversified 
investment portfolio. The Foundation model showcases a set of allocations focusing on traditional asset classes that are 
accessible to most investors, while the Found ation+ model includes allocations to private assets that may be accessible to 
investors in some jurisdictions, but not others.

2 Foundation: Our tactical asset allocation

### Page 7

Global Market Outlook 7

Summary

Artificial intelligence (AI) has become one of the most powerful drivers of global market performance and, increasingly, 
one of the largest contributors to portfolio risk.  While we remain constructive on the long -term outlook for equities and 
continue to view AI as a transformational investment theme, our tactical positioning has recently shifted from an Overweight to 
a Neutral equity weight. This reflects a more balanced near -term risk -reward profile after a period of strong equity market 
performance and increasingly concentrated market leadership. 
Against this backdrop, a deeper understanding of portfolio construction is essential. Investors should consider not only 
how much they allocate to equities, bonds and alternative investments, but also the underlying factors driving portfolio risk. Using 
a Total Portfolio Approach (TPA), we assessed the factor exposures embedded within our Foundation and Foundation+ 
Balanced portfolios, with a particular focus on AI concentration risk. 
Our analysis suggests both portfolios are more diversified than a traditional 60/40 allocation  and maintain meaningful 
exposure to long -term AI growth opportunities.  The Foundation+ Balanced portfolio provides additional diversification 
through alternative investments and a broader range of return drivers, reducing reliance on public equity markets.

Key findings

Looking beyond asset classes: Understanding 
AI concentration risk 
Investors often assess diversification through the lens of asset 
allocation. A portfolio containing equities, bonds, gold and 
alternatives may appear diversified on the surface. However, 
this traditional approach alone does not always reveal where 
portfolio risks are truly concentrated. 
TPA starts from a different premise. Rather than focusing solely 
on asset classes, it seeks to identify the underlying factors that 
drive portfolio outcomes, including equity markets, interest 
rates, credit spreads, currencies, liquidity conditions and 
thematic exposures such as AI. 
This perspective is particularly relevant following our recent 
move from an Overweight to a Neutral equity allocation. While 
we remain constructive on equities over the long term, a 
growing share of market returns has been driven by a relatively 
narrow group of AI -related beneficiaries. For investors, t he 
question is, therefore, not whether to maintain exposure to AI, 
but whether their portfolios have become overly dependent on 
a single theme or underlying risk factor.

### Page 8

Global Market Outlook 8

US equities exhibit some of the highest levels of AI intensity, 
reflecting the dominant role US companies play across the AI 
value chain. By contrast, fixed income, private assets and gold 
display significantly lower AI sensitivity and derive their 
returns from different economic drivers. 
The objective is not to eliminate AI exposure. We continue to 
believe AI will remain one of the defining structural growth 
themes of the coming decade. Instead, the objective is to 
ensure portfolios participate in this opportunity without 
becoming excessively dependent on it. 
Stronger diversification through different 
return drivers 
One of the most common misconceptions is that greater 
diversification inevitably leads to lower returns. Our analysis 
suggests the opposite may be true when diversification is 
achieved through genuinely different return drivers. Both 
Foundation and Foundation + portfolios exhibit attractive risk-
return characteristics relative to a traditional 60/40 allocation 
(see Fig. 9). They deliver comparable or higher return 
potential with lower volatility, smaller drawdowns and reduced 
sensitivity to global equity markets.

The key insight is that diversification works best when risks 
originate from different economic drivers. Simply increasing 
the number of securities or asset classes is not sufficient. 
What matters is the breadth of return sources supporting 
portfolio outcomes. 
AI remains a meaningful component of portfolio risk , and its 
exposure is spread across several parts of the ecosystem.  
Non-AI-related factors , including equity beta, credit, 
currencies and liquidity premia, also drive portfolio risk . This 
broader mix reduces dependence on any single investment 
theme and creates a more balanced portfolio structure. 
The role of alternatives: Creating additional 
return engines 
The most important distinction between our Foundation and 
Foundation+ portfolios is the inclusion of hedge funds and 
private assets in the latter. Alternatives can provide additional 
return engines and reduce reliance on equity market beta, but 
different strategies offer very different forms of diversification. 
Within hedge funds, a multi-strategy provides a strong 
starting point for investors seeking lower equity sensitivity and 
differentiated return sources, with an equity beta of around 
0.15-0.20 and a correlation slightly above 50%  to global 
equities’ performance. Equity long/short retains greater 
participation in equity-led growth, reflected in a higher 0.45 
beta, and has historically captured 56% of global equity gains. 
However, it also maintains greater common exposure to 
equity and AI factors. Relative value offers the lowest 
equity exposure, with a beta of around 0.10 and participation 
below 5% of global equity losses , although nearly 80% of its 
modelled risk is attributed to credit (partly reflecting the use of 
convertible-arbitrage trades), highlighting that reducing equity 
beta can mean substituting it with another source of risk.  
Within private markets, the sources of return also differ 
materially. Private credit offers the most differentiated 
return engine, with returns driven primarily by income and 
credit premia rather than equity appreciation. Private equity, 
by contrast, remains more closely linked to equity and 
growth factors, making it a stronger source of long-term return 
potential but a less effective tool for reducing portfolio beta. 
Infrastructure can provide an attractive middle ground. It 
retains exposure to AI -related structural themes, particularly 
through digital and power infrastructure, and offers lower equity 
beta and correlation than traditional public equities. 
Ultimately, our move from an Overweight to a Core allocation 
in equity markets does not change our constructive long-term 
view on equities or AI. Rather, it reinforces the importance of 
understanding where portfolio risk originates. Investors do not 
need to choose between participating in the AI opportunity 
and maintaining diversific ation. Using a TPA lens combines 
exposure to long -term growth themes with a broader set of 
return drivers, including carefully selected alternatives,  and 
reduces dependence on any single source of market 
performance.

### Page 9

Global Market Outlook 9

Key themes

Core scenario (soft landing, 45% probability): An economic soft landing remains our core scenario, with the global economy 
overcoming this year’s oil price shock thanks to a surge in AI investment in the US and Asia. Record wealth generation driven 
by the stock market rally is supporting US consumption. Fiscal easing in the US, Germany and Japan is supporting growth. 
Meanwhile, inflation has reaccelerated due to a rebound in fuel prices as the Middle East conflict drags on. Against this backdrop, 
the Fed, ECB and BoJ all hiked rates in the same month (in September) for the first time. Although all three subsequently paused 
in October, we expect the Fed to hike twice more, the ECB once more and the BoJ thrice more by June 2027. Nevertheless, we 
expect the energy and tariff effects on inflation to fade by H2 2027, enabling the Fed and the ECB to cut rates by Q3 2027. 
Meanwhile, China is likely to target credit easing and accelerate fiscal spending in H1 2027 to revive domestic growth.  
Upside risk (no landing, 30% probability):  We maintain the probability at 30% a s AI-driven investment growth  continues. 
There is a reasonable chance that lower oil prices next year, the AI-fuelled wealth boom and fiscal easing in Germany and Japan 
will boost ‘animal spirits’. A potential global defence spending boom, led by the US and Europe, could also lift global growth. 
Downside risk (25% probability): We raised the risk of a recession to 15%, especially if central banks are forced to accelerate 
the pace of rate hikes to douse rising inflation. A stock market downturn negating the wealth effect or a bond sell-off due to fiscal 
concerns are other tail risks. We cut stagflation probability to 10% as we see low risk of oil prices sustaining above USD 120/bbl.
